Across 26 tasks spanning classification, regression, and recommendation, the optimized graphs consistently improve accuracy while often reducing inference cost.

</details>


### [148] [Explaining Black-Box Language Models: Learning to Optimize Linguistically-Structured Word Subsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08497)
*Minyoung Hwang,Seokhyun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的黑盒可解释方法，通过选择输入词子集来解释深度语言模型的预测，同时满足推理效率、黑盒兼容性和语言结构可解释性三个关键需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度语言模型在医疗等高 stakes 领域广泛应用，但其黑盒特性（如API调用）限制了内部状态访问，现有解释方法难以同时满足推理效率、黑盒兼容且不引发分布外行为、以及基于输入语言结构的可理解解释三个关键需求。

Method: 将解释问题建模为摊销优化问题，通过REINFORCE策略梯度训练离散词选择策略，无需输入特定搜索即可实现一次性推理；同时融入图结构知识以增强语言连贯性和认知可解释性。

Result: 在多种DLM架构和真实数据集上，该方法一致识别出具有更强判别能力和语言显著性对齐的词子集，优于传统黑盒兼容方法以及可访问梯度的梯度基方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效解决了黑盒DLM解释中的三大挑战，在保持推理效率的同时生成与人类语言直觉对齐的可理解解释。

Abstract: As deep language models (DLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, understanding their decision rationale becomes paramount for ensuring trust, safety, and accountability. However, achieving this vital level of interpretability is particularly challenging when these DLMs operate as black-box systems (e.g., via APIs), where access to internal model states (e.g., parameters, gradients) is restricted. Despite numerous efforts, existing explanation methods often fail to concurrently satisfy three key desiderata: (i) inference-time efficiency, (ii) black-box compatibility without inducing out-of-distribution behavior, and (iii) comprehensible explanations grounded in the input's linguistic structure. To address these challenges, we propose a method that explains predictions of DLMs by selecting a small, informative subset of input words. We formulate this as an amortized optimization problem, enabling efficient one-shot inference without the need for input-specific search. Our selection policy is trained via REINFORCE-style policy gradients, allowing discrete word selection in a fully gradient-free setting. To enhance interpretability and align with human linguistic intuition, we integrate graph-structured knowledge into this selection process, fostering linguistically coherent subsets that result in explanations both highly informative and cognitively meaningful to end-users. We evaluated our method on diverse DLM architectures and multiple real-world datasets. It consistently identifies word subsets with enhanced discriminative power and stronger alignment with linguistically salient cues, outperforming both conventional black-box compatible methods and gradient-based approaches that are given oracle access to the black-box model's gradients for a more challenging benchmark. Our code is available at here.

</details>


### [149] [Standpoint Logics with Defeasible Beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08503)
*Nicholas Leisegang,Thomas Meyer,Sebastian Rudolph*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文融合KLM可废逻辑与立场逻辑，提出DRSL框架以表达多视角下的可废信念。通过公理化语义刻画、提升多种蕴涵关系（优先蕴涵、理性闭包、词典闭包）到含立场的场景，并证明复杂性类不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑框架难以同时处理多个（可能矛盾的）视角及其各自持有的可废信念，缺乏形式化表达多视角下可废推理的统一方案。

Method: 将KLM可废逻辑与立场逻辑结合，利用DRSL框架；为DRSL提供基于KLM风格公设的语义刻画；通过语义和算法两种等价方式将优先蕴涵、基于单排序函数的蕴涵关系（理性闭包、词典闭包）提升到含立场的语境。

Result: 建立了DRSL语义的基础表示结果；证明了每种蕴涵关系从命题KLM迁移到DRSL后，蕴涵检验的复杂度类保持不变。

Conclusion: DRSL能在多视角场景下有效地表达可废推理，且不增加计算复杂性代价，为构建兼具立场多样性与可废性的知识表示框架提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we integrate the defeasible logic of Kraus, Lehmann and Magidor (KLM) with the standpoint logic framework of Gómez Álvarez and Rudolph. This is done with the goal of formally expressing knowledge taking into account multiple (possibly contradicting) viewpoints, which in turn may hold defeasible beliefs. In doing so, we utilise Defeasible Restricted Standpoint Logics (DRSL), introduced by Leisegang et al. Our work expands on previous work by providing a foundational representation result for DRSL semantics and systematically lifting several well-known entailment relations from the propositional case to the standpoint-enhanced setting. In particular, we characterise the semantics for DRSL through a set of KLM-style postulates adapted for the standpoints case. We furthermore provide a means to lift preferential entailment, and the class of entailment relations based on single ranking functions from the purely propositional to the standpoint-enhanced context, including rational and lexicographic closure. We show this can be done equivalently through semantic and algorithmic means. Furthermore, we show that, for each considered form of entailment, the complexity class of entailment checking does not change when moving from propositional KLM to DRSL.

</details>


### [150] [Scaffold Effects on GAIA: A Controlled Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08529)
*Jason Starace*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验比较了三种脚手架（ReAct、多智能体设计、规划-执行）在多个模型上的表现，发现脚手架选择可导致准确率差异高达28个百分点，且更强大的模型对脚手架更敏感，驳斥了预注册假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体能力评分混淆了模型本身能力与脚手架带来的能力，这种"诱导差距"在受控条件下的量化研究不足。

Method: 预注册的受控比较实验：三种脚手架（ReAct、Planner-Actor-Rater多智能体设计、planner-then-executor）× 五个模型（Claude Opus 4.7/Sonnet 4.6/Haiku 4.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview、GPT-5.5）× GAIA验证集Level 1和2，每问题三次尝试，固定任务和条件。

Result: 脚手架选择导致同一模型内准确率差异高达28个百分点；预注册假设（更强大的模型对脚手架敏感度更低）被证伪——最强模型在更难层级从结构化脚手架获益最多；多智能体优势仅存在于Anthropic系列而非跨供应商模型；结构化脚手架调用工具次数更少但在更难层级错误恢复率更高；Gemini+planner-then-executor组合在成本最低的同时在Level 2准确率最高。

Conclusion: 单脚手架能力评分是脚手架条件性估计值，诱导差距并不随模型能力提升而必然缩小。

Abstract: Published agent capability scores conflate what a model can do with what its scaffold lets it do, and the magnitude of this elicitation gap is not well characterized under controlled conditions. This study executes a pre-registered controlled comparison of three scaffolds (ReAct, a Planner-Actor-Rater multi-agent design, and planner-then-executor) across five models from three providers (Claude Opus 4.7, Sonnet 4.6, Haiku 4.5; Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview; GPT-5.5) on GAIA validation Levels 1 and 2, holding tasks and conditions fixed, with three attempts per question. Scaffold choice alone moves measured accuracy by as much as 28 percentage points within a single model (Opus, Level 2, robust slice), confirming the pre-registered hypothesis that scaffold variation produces gaps of at least 10 points. The pre-registered prediction that more capable models would be less scaffold-sensitive is rejected in direction: scaffold effects vary significantly by model in every dataset slice, but the most capable Anthropic model gains the most from structured scaffolds at the harder level, and tier-scaling holds only at Level 1 under the robust slice. The multi-agent advantage over ReAct at Level 2 appears within the Anthropic family but not for the cross-provider models, making model family rather than capability tier the conditioning variable, and the predicted planner-executor advantage on file-reading tasks is falsified. Structured scaffolds make fewer tool calls yet recover more often from mid-trajectory errors at the harder level, and a single cell (Gemini with planner-then-executor) is the cheapest at both levels and the most accurate at Level 2. These results indicate that single-scaffold capability numbers are scaffold-conditional estimates and that the elicitation gap is not guaranteed to shrink as models improve.

</details>


### [151] [VESTA: A Fully Automated Scenario Generation and Safety Evaluation Framework for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08531)
*Lu Jia,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao,Jindong Li,Dongqi Liang,Ping Wu,Qian Zhang,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VESTA是一个面向LLM智能体的全自动场景生成与安全评估框架，基于五个风险维度生成1072个可执行评估场景，对12个LLM智能体在两个权威上下文下的评估显示，平均攻击成功率达到47.1%，部分模型超过70%，揭示了当前智能体在任务执行中仍存在严重的行为安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估方法依赖人工编写场景、静态提示或最终输出判断，难以捕捉智能体在任务执行过程中面临的多样化风险，亟需一种可执行的、过程级的自动化安全评估框架。

Method: VESTA框架：基于五个风险维度，将抽象安全风险实例化为1072个可测量的真实任务执行场景；构建自动化评估流水线，在两种权威上下文下对12个LLM智能体进行评估。

Result: 12个LLM智能体的平均攻击成功率(ASR)为47.1%，多个模型超过70%，表明当前智能体在任务执行中面临显著的行为安全风险。

Conclusion: 可执行、过程级的评估对理解和提升LLM智能体的安全性至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evolving from simple text-based interaction systems into LLM agents that can maintain memory, use tools, access external environments, and execute tasks. As their capabilities and autonomy expand, the safety risks they face also become more diverse. Existing evaluations often rely on manually written scenarios, static prompts, or final-output judgments, making it difficult to capture the diverse risks that agents may face during task execution. We introduce VESTA, a fully automated scenario generation and safety evaluation framework for LLM agents. Based on five risk dimensions, VESTA instantiaes abstract and diverse safety risks in real-world task execution into 1,072 measurable evaluation scenarios. Using the automated evaluation pipeline, 12 LLM agents are evaluated under two authority contexts. The results show that current agents still face substantial behavioral safety risks during task execution, with an average ASR of 47.1% and several models exceeding 70%. These findings demonstrate the importance of executable, process-level evaluation for understanding and improving LLM agent safety.

</details>


### [152] [DN-Hypo-Pipeline: An AI-Driven Workflow for Hypothesis Generation via Large Language Models and Scientific Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08532)
*Lei Lin,Ronghao Wang,Chunbao Zhou,Jue Wang,Yangang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DN-Hypo-Pipeline，一个基于大语言模型的AI工作流，通过利用科学解释作为先验知识，支持结构化科学思维和假设生成。该流程能从现有文献中推导出新假设，在数据科学建模领域通过三篇高被引论文验证，比直接生成方法更有效，且生成的两个最高分假设对应算法超越了原论文基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学假设是研究的第一步，需要经过实验验证，同时也反映了对科学现象的深度理解和推理。现有方法在利用科学解释作为先验知识进行结构化假设生成方面存在不足。

Method: 提出DN-Hypo-Pipeline工作流，基于大语言模型，以科学解释为先验知识。给定研究论文的解释项（结论），识别潜在定律、理论和原理，重构新的、待验证的对观测现象的解释。在数据科学建模领域使用三篇高被引论文进行评估，采用LLM-as-judge和人类专家评估相结合的统计推断方法。

Result: DN-Hypo-Pipeline比直接生成方法更有效；两个最高分生成假设对应的新算法超越了原论文基线模型；该方法不仅涵盖了理论引导的数据科学建模方法，还揭示了建模过程中更基础的结构。

Conclusion: DN-Hypo-Pipeline为理论引导建模提供了一般化框架，具有跨学科推广潜力，可扩展到其他科学领域。

Abstract: A scientific hypothesis is the first step in research and undergoes experimental validation, yet it also reflects a deep understanding of and reasoning about scientific phenomena. We introduce DN-Hypo-Pipeline, an AI-powered workflow based on large language models, designed to support structured scientific thinking and hypothesis generation by leveraging scientific explanations as prior knowledge. This pipeline assists researchers in deriving novel hypotheses from existing literature. Given the explanandum (i.e., the conclusion) of a research paper, it identifies underlying laws, theories, and principles, and reconstructs a new, yet-to-be-verified explanation for the observed phenomenon. We evaluated DN-Hypo-Pipeline in the field of data science modeling using three highly cited papers. Statistical inference, supported by both LLM-as-judge assessment and human expert evaluation, demonstrates that our pipeline is more effective than direct generation methods. Additionally, we validated the two highest-scoring generated hypotheses by developing corresponding novel algorithms, which outperformed the baseline models presented in the original papers. Beyond application in data science, DN-Hypo-Pipeline provides a theoretical framework that not only encompasses theory-guided data science modeling methods but also reveals a more fundamental structure of the modeling process. Moreover, this approach is essentially a generalization of theory-guided modeling, offering potential for extension to other domains and across a broader range of scientific disciplines.

</details>


### [153] [AgentTrust: A Self-Improving Trust Layer for AI-Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08539)
*Chenglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentTrust v2 提出了一种基于威胁类型（词法 vs. 语义）的双层信任架构：词法威胁由可自蒸馏的确定性规则处理，语义威胁由带自学习能力的 LLM 裁判处理，并通过守卫式 RAG 记忆和在线回放持续演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent 在执行 shell 命令、云操作等关键动作时，需要一个信任层来决策允许、警告、阻止或升级。现有方法无法有效区分词法威胁（固定签名可规则判断）和语义威胁（表面相同但意图不同，规则天然不可解），导致准确性不足。

Method: 1) 将威胁分为词法型（可规则判定）和语义型（需意图理解）；2) 用确定性规则处理词法威胁，用 LLM 裁判处理语义威胁；3) 赋予裁判自学习能力：在语义攻击为主的语料上，规则准确率仅 48%，裁判提升至 83.6-85.2%，近零误拦；4) 构建双存储系统：裁判将词法规则自蒸馏到规则库（降低成本），语义威胁存入带守卫的 RAG 记忆（验证守卫提升语义准确率 +13pp）；5) 在线回放实现裁判调用率下降、准确率上升，45,000 动作零误拦。

Result: 手工云规则包准确率仅从 48% 提升到 56%，语义类准确率无变化（data_db 29→29, observability 59→59, supply_chain 50→50）；LLM 裁判将整体准确率提升至 83.6-85.2%；守卫式 RAG 记忆使语义准确率从 70% 提升至 84%（+13pp）；在线回放中裁判调用率从 50% 降至 44%，准确率从 71% 升至 80%，45,000 动作零硬拦良性操作。

Conclusion: AgentTrust v2 证明了基于威胁类型分类的信任层设计是可行且有效的：词法威胁用可自蒸馏的规则低成本处理，语义威胁用带自学习能力的 LLM 裁判处理，并通过守卫式 RAG 记忆持续演进。系统在不误拦良性动作的前提下，实现了准确率的显著提升和成本的持续降低。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly take consequential actions -- shell commands, cloud operations, and arbitrary tool-calls -- so a trust layer must decide, per action, whether to allow, warn, block, or escalate. We argue that the right way to reason about such a layer is by threat type. Lexical (fixed-signature) threats, where danger lives in a stable token, are decidable by deterministic rules; semantic (intent-dependent) threats, where a benign and a malicious action share the same surface, are out of reach for rules by construction. We make this concrete with a negative proof: a determined, hand-authored cloud rule pack lifts held-out accuracy only 48 to 56% overall and moves the semantic categories by 0pp (data_db 29 to 29, observability 59 to 59, supply_chain 50 to 50), while a strong LLM judge carries exactly those categories. We give the judge a self-learning capability: on a corpus that is mainly semantic attacks it nearly doubles rule accuracy (48% to 83.6-85.2%) with near-zero false-blocks, and this holds across two model providers. We turn this into a self-improving dual-store system: the judge distills a growing deterministic rule floor on lexical threats (cheaper over time) and feeds a guarded RAG memory on semantic threats (a verdict-cache fails -- surface-twins collapse to ~58% -- so a corroboration guard lifts semantic accuracy +13pp, 70 to 84). The result is what sets AgentTrust v2 apart from its static v1 predecessor: a trust layer that self-evolves from its own stream of decisions -- cheaper on the lexical class (it distils its own rules) and smarter on the semantic class (it accrues guarded precedent), while never hard-blocking a benign action. An end-to-end online replay shows the judge-call rate falling (50% to 44%) and judge-domain accuracy rising (71% to 80%), with 0 benign hard-blocks across 45,000 actions.

</details>


### [154] [PAEC: Position-Aware Entropy Calibration for LLM Reasoning in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08543)
*Shumeng Yang,Yisu Liu,Jiayi Zheng,Zhaohui Yang,Linjing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出位置感知熵校准（PAEC）框架，通过局部top-p熵和top-2候选竞争构建软掩码，对决策敏感位置施加锚定下限惩罚，防止RLVR中策略熵过早崩溃，在数学推理基准上提升了多数投票性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练中策略熵迅速崩溃，导致推理路径过早收敛到少数高概率路径；全局熵正则化在长推理轨迹中效率低下，因为许多token位置并非决策关键点。

Method: 提出PAEC框架：1) 基于局部top-p熵和top-2候选竞争构建软掩码，识别决策敏感位置；2) 对这些位置施加锚定下界惩罚，防止熵崩溃，同时不影响非关键位置的探索。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准上，PAEC相比强RLVR基线提升了宏观平均多数投票性能，在AIME类任务上增益显著。

Conclusion: 推理RL中的熵管理应被形式化为对决策敏感位置的选择性探索分配，而非均匀随机性注入。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves large language model reasoning but often suffers from rapid policy-entropy collapse, where the policy prematurely concentrates on narrow high-probability reasoning paths. While global entropy regularization can encourage exploration, uniformly increasing entropy across all token positions is inefficient for long reasoning trajectories, where many tokens are not decision-relevant. We propose Position-Aware Entropy Calibration (PAEC), a token-level entropy-management framework that constructs a soft mask from local top-p entropy and top-two candidate competition, and applies an anchor-based lower-bound penalty to prevent selected-position entropy collapse. Experiments on five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that PAEC improves macro-average majority-vote performance over strong RLVR baselines, with clear gains on AIME-style tasks. Our results suggest that entropy management in reasoning RL should be formulated as selective exploration allocation over decision-sensitive positions rather than uniform randomness injection.

</details>


### [155] [Quantitative Promise Theory: Intentionality and Inference in Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08552)
*Mark Burgess*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文讨论了Promise Theory在自主代理系统中的定量表示，包括如何将贝叶斯概率、信息论优化（如Active Inference）与承诺语义相结合，以及Promise Theory如何帮助避免概率计算的陷阱（如非局部协调、校准和归一化）。边界条件作为一种承诺形式约束了允许状态和决策阈值，而代理对齐提供了意图的可扩展定义。自主代理可通过最小化信息来凝聚成具有超代理特征的集群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Promise Theory的定量表示引入涉及自主代理的系统中，解决传统概率方法在处理非局部协调、校准和归一化等问题时的局限性，提供一种补充性解决方案。

Method: 通过将贝叶斯概率和信息论优化（包括Active Inference）与Promise Theory的承诺语义相结合，利用边界条件作为约束状态和决策阈值的承诺形式，以及代理对齐作为意图的可扩展定义。

Result: Promise Theory能够补充概率方法，避免其非局部协调、校准和归一化等陷阱；边界条件作为承诺约束允许状态；代理对齐提供可扩展的意图定义；自主代理通过最小化信息聚合成具有超级代理特征的集群。

Conclusion: Promise Theory为自主代理系统提供了有价值的定量框架，但与概率方法的整合仍面临研究挑战和风格偏好问题。

Abstract: I discuss some quantitative representations of Promise Theory for processes involving autonomous agents. Agent models are common in software systems, machine learning, and biology, for example, but may also apply to physics and other forms of engineering. I describe how Bayesian probability and information theoretic optimization, including Active Inference, may be incorporated with promise semantics -- as well as how Promise Theory supplements solutions, helping to avoid probability's pitfalls, which include non-local coordination, calibrating, and normalizing probabilistic computations. The role of boundary conditions in constraining allowed states and selecting decision thresholds is a form of promise, and agent alignment provides a scalable definition of intent. Autonomous agents may congeal into swarms with superagent characteristics by trying to minimize their information, despite uncertainty that works to maximize it. The use of Promise Theory involves some research challenges as well as stylistic preferences.

</details>


### [156] [Distilling LLM Reasoning into an Interpretable Policy Tree for Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08596)
*Beiwen Zhang,Yongheng Liang,Guowei Zou,Haitao Wang,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Co-pi-tree方法，通过将LLM推理蒸馏为可执行的策略树代码，并结合闭环反馈机制进行迭代优化，在Overcooked-AI任务中平均奖励提升35.4%，LLM查询次数减少77.7%，测试时延降低97.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖多智能体强化学习学习黑盒策略，缺乏可解释性且存在安全隐患；要么在每个决策步骤查询大语言模型，导致响应慢、推理成本高。需要一种兼具可解释性、高效性和安全性的协作策略学习方法。

Method: 提出Co-pi-tree闭环方法：（1）将LLM推理蒸馏为可执行的策略树代码，包含伙伴行为预测树和智能体动作选择树；（2）通过与伙伴交互评估策略并获取反馈；（3）使用自然语言总结交互反馈，改进问题分支。

Result: 在Overcooked-AI环境中，平均奖励提升35.4%，LLM查询次数减少77.7%，测试时延降低97.1%。

Conclusion: Co-pi-tree通过将LLM推理蒸馏为策略树并结合闭环反馈优化，在提升协作性能的同时大幅降低了计算开销和延迟，实现了可解释、高效的人机协作策略学习。

Abstract: Constructing efficient and reliable policies to assist humans is indispensable for human-AI collaboration. Existing methods mainly follow two lines of work. Most prior work relies on multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) to learn black-box policies, which limits interpretability and raises safety concerns. Recent methods query large language models (LLMs) at each decision step, causing slow responses and high inference costs. We propose Collaboration Policy Tree (Co-pi-tree), a closed-loop method that learns an executable policy tree consisting of a partner-behavior prediction tree and an agent-action selection tree. Co-pi-tree constructs a policy by distilling LLM reasoning into policy tree code. It then evaluates the policy through partner interaction, obtains feedback, and uses natural language to summarize the interaction feedback to improve problematic branches. Experiments in Overcooked-AI show that Co-pi-tree improves average reward by 35.4% over the baseline average, while reducing the number of LLM queries by 77.7% and test-time latency by 97.1%. Project page: https://beiwenzhang.github.io/Co-pi-tree/

</details>


### [157] [InA-Probe: Instruction-Aware Active Probing for Time Series Forecasting with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08601)
*Peiliang Gong,Emadeldeen Eldele,Chenyu Liu,Ziyu Jia,Yi Ding,Xinliang Zhou,Lianchao Gu,Qi Zhu,Yang Liu,Daoqiang Zhang,Xiaoli Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种指令感知主动探测（InA-Probe）方法，通过多层级指令注入、自适应查询生成和双阶段注意力机制，将LLM从被动对齐转向主动探测，显著提升时间序列预测性能，在跨域场景中误差降低高达37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM时间序列预测方法主要依赖被动模态对齐或静态任务重编程，难以捕捉细粒度、非平稳的时间模式，也无法适应细微的任务意图差异。

Method: 设计多层级指令注入机制（全局任务目标+细粒度patch级语义先验），结合自适应查询生成模块生成样本特定的探测向量，通过指令感知自注意力（内化任务意图）和时序交叉注意力（提取显著模式）的双阶段注意力过程进行精炼。

Result: 在7个真实世界基准上持续优于SOTA深度学习和LLM基线，在跨域场景中预测误差降低高达37%，在one-for-all泛化和零样本迁移方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 自适应查询和细粒度指令的协同作用是解锁LLM在复杂时间序列预测中推理能力的关键。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated impressive potential for time series forecasting. However, existing methods predominantly rely on passive modality alignment or static task reprogramming, which often fail to capture fine-grained, non-stationary temporal patterns or to adapt to nuanced task intents. In this paper, we propose Instruction-aware Active Probing (InA-Probe), which shifts the paradigm from passive alignment toward an active, instruction-driven probing mechanism. Specifically, we design a Multi-Level Instruction Injection mechanism that enriches the model with both global task objectives and fine-grained, patch-level semantic priors. Building on this, an Adaptive Query Generation module produces sample-specific probes that are dynamically modulated by the temporal context. These probes are then refined through a dual-stage attention process: they first internalize task-specific intents via Instruction-Aware Self-Attention, and subsequently interrogate the projected temporal representations through Temporal Cross-Attention to extract salient patterns. Comprehensive experiments on seven real-world benchmarks show that InA-Probe consistently outperforms state-of-the-art deep learning and LLM-based baselines, excelling in both one-for-all generalization and zero-shot transfer while reducing forecasting error by up to 37\% in challenging cross-domain scenarios. Ablation studies further confirm that the synergy between adaptive querying and fine-grained instructions is key to unlocking the reasoning power of LLMs for complex time series.

</details>


### [158] [Towards Long-Horizon Vessel Trajectory and Destination Forecasting with Reasoning Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08633)
*Hongwei Wang,Miao Zhou,Fengde Wang,Yuting Wang,Jiewen Yu,Jun-Yan He,Bohao Qu,Wanbing Zhang,Xiuju Fu,Qing Guo,Zipei Fan,Yingying Xing,Yi Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）的海事LLM后训练框架，用于长时域（30天）船舶轨迹与目的地联合预测。通过将AIS轨迹转化为语义文本表示，RLVR框架利用物理有效性约束、早期加权轨迹监督及分层匹配课程学习来优化LLM，实验表明4B LLM取得最优性能，优于8B/14B模型及传统深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域（月级别）船舶轨迹预测对航运管理、物流规划及风险分析至关重要，但现有深度学习主要集中在短中期坐标外推，难以在长时间跨度下保证航线可行性与目的地正确性，因此需要探索具备推理能力的LLM在该任务上的应用。

Method: 构建基于AIS数据的60天历史轨迹+30天预测基准，将轨迹转化为语义文本表示用于RL提示构建；采用RLVR框架，通过物理有效性约束、早期加权轨迹监督及分层匹配课程学习对LLM进行后训练对齐。

Result: RLVR训练的LLM在目的地相关指标上显著优于零样本LLM和代表性深度学习基线；4B LLM取得最佳综合性能，优于8B/14B模型；LSTM在有限微调数据下仍是强基线，而Transformer类模型需要更多数据和结构化输入。

Conclusion: 本文推动了对齐可验证奖励的语义化海事预测，表明奖励兼容优化与任务特定容量匹配比单纯扩大模型规模更为重要，为长时域轨迹预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon maritime trajectory prediction is important for shipping management, logistics planning, and maritime risk analysis, yet month-level forecasting remains insufficiently studied. Existing deep learning methods mainly focus on short- and mid-term coordinate extrapolation and often struggle to preserve route feasibility and destination correctness over extended horizons. This paper investigates joint long-horizon vessel trajectory and destination forecasting with reasoning-capable large language models, and develops a Maritime LLM post-training framework based on Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR). An AIS-based benchmark is constructed with 60-day historical trajectories and 30-day forecasting horizons, where trajectories are converted into semantic textual representations for RL prompt construction. RLVR aligns LLMs with maritime forecasting objectives by enforcing physical validity, providing early-weighted trajectory supervision, and evaluating destination correctness through hierarchical matching and curriculum learning. Experimental results show that RLVR-trained LLMs substantially improve over zero-shot LLMs and representative deep learning baselines, especially on destination-related metrics. Among the evaluated RLVR-trained variants, 4B LLMs achieve the best overall performance, suggesting that reward-compatible optimization and task-specific capacity matching are more important than simply using larger 8B or 14B LLMs. The results also show that LSTM remains a strong deep learning baseline under limited fine-tuning data, while Transformer-style spatio-temporal models typically require larger datasets and richer structured inputs. Overall, this work advances semantic, verifier-aligned maritime forecasting for operational decision support.

</details>


### [159] [Extending Ontologies: From Dense Embeddings to Hybrid Quantum-Fuzzy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08658)
*Angjelin Hila*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了本体论和知识图谱与密集嵌入算法的集成方式，指出所有现有方法都在概率推理和精确推理之间存在权衡。为此，论文提出了一种新的前沿方向——神经-量子-模糊系统，通过量子神经网络（QNN）实现既能容纳经典推理又能进行上下文推理的知识表示系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs虽然革新了知识表示和检索，但缺乏本体论所具有的显式建模能力。现有方法无法在同一表示中同时兼顾概率推理和精确推理，存在本质上的权衡。

Method: 提出神经-量子-模糊系统（neuro-quantum-fuzzy systems），利用量子神经网络（QNN）实现同时支持经典推理和上下文推理的知识表示系统。

Result: 提出了一种融合本体论、知识图谱与密集嵌入算法的新框架，能够同时处理概率推理和精确推理。

Conclusion: 神经-量子-模糊系统为解决概率推理与精确推理的权衡问题提供了新的可行方向，有望构建更强大的知识表示系统。

Abstract: LLMs have revolutionized knowledge representation and retrieval, but lack the explicit modeling that knowledge ontologies possess. This paper surveys the ways that ontologies and knowledge graphs have been integrated with dense embedding algorithms. All hitherto attempts involve a trade-off between probabilistic and crisp inference. This paper proposes a novel frontier for devising knowledge representation systems that can simultaneously accommodate probabilistic and crisp inference in the same representation. To this effect, the paper proposes neuro-quantum-fuzzy systems as knowledge representation systems that accommodate both classical and contextual inference implemented through quantum-neural networks (QNN).

</details>


### [160] [ConMem: Structured Memory-Guided Adaptation in Training-Free Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08702)
*Zhixun Tan,Qiang Chen,Tairan Huang,Xiu Su,Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConMem是一个无需额外训练、关系感知的多智能体系统框架，通过结构化记忆卡和关系图实现跨经验协调，在多个基准测试中优于现有记忆架构，同时剪枝50%以上候选并减少80%规划开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统虽通过记忆、技能和学习方法提升了自适应能力，但仍面临噪声轨迹、记忆-技能关系建模不足以及依赖额外训练或高质量监督等挑战。

Method: ConMem将历史交互轨迹提炼为结构化记忆卡，捕捉可复用策略和线索，并组织成关系感知的记忆图；运行时根据任务需求检索记忆卡，通过卡片图协调以解决策略冲突并恢复依赖关系。

Result: 在多个基准和主流MAS架构上取得一致性增益，推理效率提升：剪枝超过50%的扩展候选，规划开销降低80%以上。

Conclusion: ConMem实现了无需额外训练的轻量级、鲁棒的多智能体自适应，验证了结构化和关系感知指导的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances have improved the adaptive capabilities of LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) through memory-, skill-, and learning-based approaches, yet these approaches remain challenged by noisy trajectories, insufficient modeling of memory-skill relations, and reliance on additional training or high-quality supervision. To address these limitations, we propose ConMem, a relation-aware and training-free framework that enables efficient multi-agent adaptation through cross-experience coordination. Specifically, ConMem distills historical interaction trajectories into structured memory cards to capture reusable strategies and cues, organizing them into a relation-aware memory graph. At runtime, ConMem retrieves cards according to task needs and coordinates them through the card graph to resolve strategy conflicts and recover their dependencies. Combined, these modules yield structured and relation-aware guidance, enabling robust, lightweight adaptation in multi-agent systems without additional training. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks and mainstream MAS architectures show consistent gains over existing memory architectures, with improved inference-time efficiency through pruning more than 50% of expanded candidates and reducing planning overhead by over 80%. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ConMemCode

</details>


### [161] [Artificial Intelligence for Mathematical Reasoning: An Integrated Survey of Language Models, Neuro-symbolic Systems, and Verified Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08728)
*Syed Rifat Raiyan,Mohsinul Kabir,Hasan Mahmud,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了数学推理在AI领域的发展历程，从早期的规则式应用题求解器到当代的推理模型、多智能体系统和神经符号定理证明器，沿四个维度（非正式推理、形式推理、数学发现、训练与推理技术）梳理了该领域的进展、基准、失败模式及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理长期以来是机器智能的严格检验标准，且已从NLP中的小众问题发展为AI最重要的前沿之一，亟需一个统一的综述来梳理该领域的演进脉络、技术分类和开放挑战。

Method: 采用四轴分类法组织文献：（i）基于文本和图的非正式推理（MWP求解、多模态几何、VLM）；（ii）形式推理（自动形式化、策略预测、编译器引导修复、证明搜索）；（iii）数学发现（构造新结果、改进界限、辅助攻克开放问题）；（iv）推理与训练技术（CoT、工具使用、过程奖励模型、RLVR）。同时系统梳理了基准数据集、评估指标和失败模式。

Result: 目录化整理了从小学校算术到竞赛数学、几何、形式证明、多模态推理等多类基准，分析了基准饱和、污染、报告不匹配以及pass@1、多数投票、验证器辅助pass@k等指标的区别；识别了脆弱性、奖励黑客、多模态接地失败、形式化脆弱性和推理能耗等关键失败模式。

Conclusion: 未来方向集中在验证驱动的发现工作流、推理效率提升以及使AI辅助形式化广泛可用的基础设施上，结合数学家的视角指出了该领域的核心挑战和机遇。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning has long served as a stringent test of machine intelligence; over the past decade, it has moved from a niche problem within NLP to one of the most consequential AI frontiers. This survey provides a unified account of the field's evolution, from early rule-based math word problem (MWP) solvers and template-driven geometry systems, through neural expression generation and LLM prompting, to contemporary reasoning models, multi-agent systems, neuro-symbolic theorem provers, and verified discovery workflows. We organize the landscape along four axes: (i) informal reasoning over text and diagrams, spanning MWP solving, multimodal geometry, and VLMs; (ii) formal reasoning in proof assistants, including autoformalization, tactic prediction, compiler-guided repair, and proof search; (iii) mathematical discovery, where systems propose constructions, improve bounds, or assist attacks on open problems; and (iv) the inference and training-time techniques, including CoT prompting, tool use, process reward models, and RLVR, that increasingly connect generation with verification. We catalog major benchmarks across grade-school arithmetic, competition mathematics, geometry, formal proving, multimodal and multilingual reasoning, and expert evaluation, and we examine benchmark saturation, contamination, reporting mismatches, and the distinction between pass@1, majority voting, and verifier-assisted pass@$k$. We critically assess failure modes: brittleness under perturbation, reward hacking, multimodal grounding failures, fragile formalization, and the energy cost of reasoning-scale inference. Drawing on recent perspectives from working mathematicians, we identify future directions centered on verified-discovery workflows, reasoning efficiency, and infrastructure to make AI-assisted formalization broadly usable. Companion materials: https://github.com/Starscream-11813/awesome-AI4Math.

</details>


### [162] [Structure-Conditioned Actor-Critic Branches for Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08735)
*Lianrong Zuo,Peilan Xu,Yong Liu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结构-价值耦合框架SV-QD-RL，将每个候选策略表示为结构条件化的演员-评论家分支，通过结构掩码、分支特定评论家和重放状态实现策略行为的专业化与多样性，在MuJoCo任务上提升了策略库的质量和行为多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QD-RL方法主要在轨迹评估后对策略实例进行多样化，或在价值学习层面改进策略质量和行为定位，但生成候选策略的学习分支本身尚未被充分探索。

Method: SV-QD-RL将每个候选策略表示为一个结构条件化的演员-评论家分支，包含演员、结构掩码、分支特定评论家、重放状态及行为、回报、稀疏性和价值画像等评估属性。结构掩码定义分支的演员子空间，分支特定评论家和重放状态塑造其价值学习轨迹，分支感知的QD存档根据行为质量、结构足迹和价值画像信息评估和保留分支。

Result: 在MuJoCo连续控制任务上，SV-QD-RL构建的策略库具有强存档质量和行为上有用的多样性。消融分析表明结构条件化、评论家差异化和记忆一致性精炼对行为专业化有互补贡献。

Conclusion: 将演员结构与分支特定价值学习耦合是生成多样化QD-RL策略库的有效机制，学习到的存档可在行为需求变化时提供可选的策略替代方案。

Abstract: Quality-diversity reinforcement learning (QD-RL) aims to construct policy repertoires that contain both high-performing and behaviorally diverse policies. Existing QD-RL methods mainly diversify policy instances after rollout evaluation or use learned value information to improve policy quality and behavior targeting, while the learning branches that generate candidate policies remain less explored. This paper proposes SV-QD-RL, a structure-value coupled framework that represents each candidate as a structure-conditioned actor-critic branch. Each branch contains an actor, a structural mask, a branch-specific critic, a replay state, and evaluation attributes including behavior, return, sparsity, and value profile. The structural mask defines the actor subspace in which the branch learns, while the branch-specific critic and replay state shape its value-learning trajectory. A branch-aware QD archive then evaluates and retains branches according to behavioral quality, structural footprint, and value-profile information. Experiments on MuJoCo continuous-control tasks show that SV-QD-RL constructs policy repertoires with strong archive quality and behaviorally useful diversity. Ablation and diagnostic analyses further indicate that structural conditioning, critic differentiation, and memory-consistent refinement make complementary contributions to behavioral specialization. Schedule-aware repertoire evaluation shows that the learned archive provides selectable policy alternatives under changing behavior-level requirements. These results suggest that coupling actor structure with branch-specific value learning is an effective mechanism for generating diverse QD-RL policy repertoires.

</details>


### [163] [RAILS: Verification-Native Clearing For Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08790)
*Adrian de Valois-Franklin,Alex Bogdan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAILS是一个面向自主智能体商务的完整性清算层，通过七原语和可验证的可靠性评分机制，解决了智能体在履行义务、责任归属和结算行动等环节缺乏中立裁决机制的问题，并首次提出了可证伪的健全性属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体系统（如谈判、采购、代码部署、资金转移）缺乏中立机制来判断智能体是否履行了被委托的义务、未履行时谁应负责以及后续结算行动。现有工具协议（MCP）、通信协议（A2A）、支付轨道（x402）、授权协议（AP2、Visa、Mastercard）和结算风险标准都假设存在这种裁决机制，但实际上都不提供。清算（clearing）是缺失的基元。

Method: 提出了RAILS（实时智能体完整性与账本结算）层，包含三个部分：每个输出的可靠性评分、公开的可靠性记录、以及消耗它们的清算函数。核心是清算协议，由七个原语组成：义务对象（Obligation Object）、证据信封（Evidence Envelope）、验证网格（Verification Mesh）、清算决策（Clearing Decision）、结算指令（Settlement Instruction）、清算通行证（Clearing Passport）、终局规则（Finality Rules）。这些原语受形式化的可接受性分级验证模型约束。

Result: 实现了一个健全性属性：任何具有财务重要性的结算都不能由低于义务可接受性底线的证据支持。该属性可针对规范进行证伪。论文作者未发现先前的智能体商务验证机制能陈述此类属性，最接近的方案仅输出通过/不通过、交付保证、原始分数或均衡状态。

Conclusion: RAILS填补了智能体商务中清算这一缺失的基元，为自主智能体系统提供了首个可验证的、具备形式化健全性保证的完整性与清算层。该协议指定了如何判定智能体是否履行义务、确定责任归属以及执行后续结算行动，使智能体商务具备中立、可验证的清算机制。

Abstract: Autonomous agents negotiate, purchase, deploy code, and move funds, but no neutral mechanism determines whether they
  met their delegated obligation, who is responsible when they did not, or which settlement action follows. This is the
  agentic clearing problem. Tool protocols (MCP), inter-agent communication (A2A), payment rails (x402), mandate and
  network agent protocols (AP2, Visa, Mastercard), and settlement-risk standards each assume that determination and none
  produce it.
  Clearing is the missing primitive. Payment is not clearing. Authorization is not clearing. LLM-as-judge evaluation is
  not clearing. Settlement-risk escrow is not clearing: it consumes clearing decisions.
  RAILS (Real-Time Agent Integrity & Ledger Settlement) is the integrity and clearing layer for agentic commerce,
  spanning a per-output reliability score, a published reliability record, and a clearing function that consumes them.
  The clearing protocol at its core closes that gap. Seven primitives (Obligation Object, Evidence Envelope,
  Verification Mesh, Clearing Decision, Settlement Instruction, Clearing Passport, Finality Rules), bound by a formal
  model of admissibility-graded verification, together yield a soundness property: no financially material settlement is
  supported by evidence below the obligation's admissibility floor. The property is falsifiable against the spec. We
  are not aware of a prior agent-commerce verification mechanism that states a property of this kind. The approaches
  nearest to it emit a pass, a delivery guarantee, a bare score, or an equilibrium.
  This paper specifies that clearing protocol.

</details>


### [164] [Bridging Expert Knowledge and Automated Feature Engineering via Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08800)
*Varun Khurana,Vijval Ekbote,Vashu Chauhan,Yaman Kumar Singla,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FEST（自进化树特征工程），通过双流特征生成、语义去重和树引导迭代进化，从原始文本和图像中发现可审计特征。在品牌分类、内容真实性检测和压力检测任务中，FEST在20个分类器-任务组合中领先17个，平均比最强基线高4.2个百分点。专家评估显示FEST特征覆盖60-80%的专家设计特征，结合专家指南可将准确率提升6-12个百分点。同时发布了首个配对专家设计特征与百万级资产的BrandGuide数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险场景（品牌合规、临床护理、内容审核）中，机器学习模型不能作为黑箱使用，从业者需要检查驱动模型决策的特征，且模型必须利用专家文档知识。现有方法针对表格输入、缺乏专家对齐、无法将定性准则（如"保持专业语气")转化为精确特征。

Method: FEST包含三个核心组件：1) 双流特征生成（语义流和确定性流）；2) 语义去重；3) 树引导的迭代进化，从原始文本和图像中发现可审计特征。

Result: 在20个分类器-任务组合中领先17个，平均增益4.2个百分点；LLM评估显示60-80%的专家设计品牌特征覆盖率；结合专家指南后准确率提升6-12个百分点；人类专家研究验证特征在相关性、清晰度和可操作性上评分高。

Conclusion: 通过将特征工程建立在专家知识基础上，FEST为需要人工监督的可解释机器学习领域开辟了一条实用路径。

Abstract: In high-stakes settings such as brand compliance, clinical care, and content moderation, machine learning cannot be deployed as opaque oracles: practitioners inspect the features driving model decisions, and models must leverage the expert documentation governing these domains. In practice, the data arrives as unstructured content, and features extracted from it must be interpretable, discriminative, and aligned with what experts consider important. Existing methods fall short: they target tabular inputs, lack demonstrated expert alignment, and cannot operationalize qualitative criteria such as 'maintain professional tone' into precise features. We present FEST (Feature Engineering with Self-evolving Trees), combining dual-stream feature generation (semantic and deterministic), semantic deduplication, and tree-guided iterative evolution to discover auditable features from raw text and images. FEST leads in 17 of 20 classifier-task combinations across brand classification, content authenticity detection, and stress detection, with a mean gain of 4.2 pp over the strongest baseline across five classifiers. An LLM-as-judge evaluation shows FEST achieves 60-80% coverage of expert-designed brand features at strict semantic-alignment thresholds, corroborated by a human expert study rating features highly on relevance, clarity, and actionability. When seeded with expert guidelines, FEST refines qualitative criteria into operational features, improving accuracy by 6-12 pp on average across brands. To enable systematic evaluation of expert alignment in automated feature engineering, we release BrandGuide, the first dataset pairing expert-designed features with 1M+ assets across 2,683 brands. By grounding feature engineering in expert knowledge, FEST opens a practical pathway for interpretable ML in domains demanding human oversight.

</details>


### [165] [Q-Delta: Beyond Key-Value Associative State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08804)
*Sumin Park,Seojin Kim,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Delta，一种查询感知的delta规则，通过将混合键-查询预测误差整合到状态演化中，在保持delta规则效率的同时实现联合纠正动态，在语言建模和长上下文检索任务上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力方法将查询限制为只读操作，使其与状态演化解耦。作者发现查询条件的状态读取能在累积记忆上产生结构化的值预测，补充基于键的检索，但现有方法未能利用这一互补关系来改进状态更新。

Method: 提出Q-Delta，一种查询感知的delta规则，将混合键-查询预测误差整合到状态演化中。建立了所得动态的稳定性保证，并推导了硬件高效的块状并行公式，使用自定义Triton实现。

Result: 稳定优化、有竞争力的吞吐量，在语言建模和长上下文检索任务上持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: 查询感知的状态更新机制能有效提升线性注意力性能，且Q-Delta在保持效率的同时实现了与delta规则相当的纠错能力。

Abstract: Linear attention reformulates sequence modeling as recurrent state evolution, enabling efficient linear-time inference. Under the key-value associative paradigm, existing approaches restrict the role of the query to the readout operation, decoupling it from state evolution. We show that query-conditioned state readout induces a structured value prediction over accumulated memory that complements key-based retrieval. Based on this insight, we propose Q-Delta, a query-aware delta rule that integrates mixed key-query prediction errors into state evolution, enabling jointly corrective dynamics while preserving delta-rule efficiency. We establish stability guarantees for the resulting dynamics and derive a hardware-efficient chunkwise-parallel formulation with a custom Triton implementation. Empirical results demonstrate stable optimization, competitive throughput, and consistent improvements over strong baselines on language modeling and long-context retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [166] [STAR: Rethinking MoE Routing as Structure-Aware Subspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08814)
*Sumin Park,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STAR通过广义Hebbian算法学习的主子空间增强路由决策，使MoE路由能感知输入结构，实现稳定的专家专业化，在合成数据、语言和视觉任务上均优于强基线方法，并在测试时通过子空间更新增强分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE的输入-专家专业化核心依赖于路由器对输入结构的感知能力，但现有浅层线性投影路由对输入表示的感知有限，导致路由不稳定，阻碍了真正的专家专业化。

Method: 将MoE路由重新视为子空间学习问题，在标准可学习路由基础上，通过广义Hebbian算法（GHA）引入一个演化的主子空间来跟踪输入的主导结构，使路由决策与输入结构对齐。

Result: 在受控合成实验、大规模语言和视觉任务上，STAR一致提升了路由质量和下游性能；测试时子空间更新进一步增强了对输入分布偏移的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过子空间学习显式建模输入结构是实现MoE稳定专家专业化的有效途径，且测试时自适应能力使其更具实用性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales model capacity efficiently by selectively routing inputs to a specialized subset of experts. However, input-expert specialization, the core motivation of MoE, critically depends on whether the router is actually aware of input structure. In practice, MoE routing is typically implemented as a shallow linear projection with limited awareness of input representation, which often leads to unstable routing. We propose STAR, a Structure Aware Routing that rethinks MoE routing as a subspace learning problem by augmenting standard learnable routing with an evolving principal subspace that tracks dominant input structure via Generalized Hebbian Algorithm (GHA). By aligning routing decisions directly with input structure, STAR enables stable expert specialization. We evaluate STAR on controlled synthetic setup and large-scale language and vision tasks, where it consistently improves routing quality and downstream performance over strong MoE baselines. Moreover, optional test-time subspace updates further enhance routing robustness and generalization under input distribution shifts.

</details>


### [167] [Momentum for Reasoning: Dense Intrinsic Signals in Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08815)
*Hao Chen,Zhanming Shen,Liyao Li,Yanyu Chen,Xuhang Zhu,Xiaomeng Hu,Qi Zhang,Ru Peng,Xiaoyu Shen,Haobo Wang,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ISPO方法，通过内在信号密化奖励机制解决GRPO在长链推理中的零优势坍缩和幻觉确定性两种失效模式，在多个数学推理基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GRPO的RLVR方法依赖二元结果奖励，存在两种结构性问题：所有rollout结果相同时梯度消失的零优势坍缩，以及模型对错误rollout过度自信的幻觉确定性，需要更细粒度的奖励信号来克服这些缺陷。

Method: 提出ISPO（内在信号策略优化），将奖励密化为两个维度：(1)序列级信号，衡量推理轨迹对最终答案的信息量；(2)词元级方向性奖励，在关键决策词元上对自信错误预测施加惩罚（幻觉确定性铰链损失）。内在信号完全从策略自身的条件概率中计算，无需外部模型。

Result: 在3个基座模型和5个数学推理基准上，ISPO一致优于基线方法，在零优势坍缩最频繁的困难基准上提升最大，训练动态诊断证实两种失效模式均被有效缓解。

Conclusion: 通过内在信号密化奖励可以有效解决GRPO的结构性失效，为RLVR提供更稳定高效的长链推理训练范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for eliciting long-chain reasoning in large language models. However, existing methods based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) rely on a binary outcome reward, which induces two structural failure modes: Zero-Advantage Collapse, in which all rollouts in a group share the same outcome and the gradient vanishes, and Hallucinated Certainty, in which the model becomes increasingly confident on incorrect rollouts late in training. We address both modes by densifying the reward with intrinsic signals computed entirely from the policy's own conditional probabilities, and propose ISPO (Intrinsic Signal Policy Optimization, which combines a sequence-level signal measuring how informative the thinking trajectory is for the final answer, with a token-level directional reward whose hallucinated-certainty hinge penalizes confidently-wrong predictions at critical decision tokens. Across three base models and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ISPO consistently outperforms competitive baselines, with the largest gains on the hardest benchmarks where zero-advantage collapse is most frequent, and training-dynamics diagnostics confirm that both failure modes are decreased.

</details>


### [168] [Inference-Time Conformal Reasoning with Valid Factuality Control for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08831)
*Ting Wang,Yuanjie Shi,Yan Yan,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出推理时共形推理（ITCR）框架，在推理图生成过程中直接集成共形预测，通过结构级事实不确定性函数聚合信号，基于图级非一致性分数校准阈值以决定何时停止生成，实现事实性的有效控制与覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测（CP）虽能提供灵活的用户指定事实性控制，但只能事后应用而无法在生成过程中进行干预；LLM多步推理中中间声明的正确性在结构上依赖于其祖先节点，导致事实不确定性具有结构性，需要推理时的不确定性量化。

Method: 提出ITCR框架：学习结构级事实不确定性函数聚合推理图上声明级事实性信号；基于图级事实不确定性设计非一致性分数；校准共形阈值以决定何时停止生成；理论上证明生成是嵌套的，保证有效的覆盖保证。

Result: 在多个数据集和覆盖目标上实验证明了经验上有效的覆盖；在下游推理任务中，推理时校准的图比事后修剪的图生成更准确。

Conclusion: ITCR框架成功弥合了CP灵活性与事后局限性之间的差距，将共形预测直接集成到推理图生成中，实现了推理时的事实性控制，并提高了下游推理任务的生成准确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly perform multi-step reasoning, where intermediate claims form implicit directed acyclic graphs whose node correctness is structurally conditioned on their ancestors. This makes factuality uncertainty structural, rather than a trivial accumulation of node-wise errors, and necessitates inference-time uncertainty quantification over the reasoning structure. While conformal prediction (CP) offers flexible user-specified factuality control, existing work remains post-hoc and cannot intervene during generation. To fill the gap between CP's flexibility and its post-hoc limitation, we propose an \emph{Inference-Time Conformal Reasoning (ITCR)} framework that integrates CP directly into reasoning graph generation. ITCR learns a structure-level factuality uncertainty function that aggregates claim-level factuality signals over reasoning graphs without complex modeling assumptions. We then design the non-conformity score based on graph-level factuality uncertainty and calibrate the conformal threshold to decide when to stop generation. We theoretically show such generation is nested, yielding valid coverage guarantees for factuality control. Experiments over multiple datasets and coverage objectives demonstrate empirically valid coverage. In downstream reasoning tasks, inference-time calibrated graphs yield more accurate generation than post-hoc pruned graphs.

</details>


### [169] [Instrumental convergence and power-seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08832)
*David Thorstad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了"AI可能因权力寻求行为而带来生存风险"这一论点依赖于强版本的"工具性收敛论题"，但现有辩护均未能充分支持该强版本论题，进而质疑了相关长期主义、AI治理及风险研究方法论的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，越来越多的人担忧AI可能很快对人类构成生存性风险，核心论据之一是智能体可能追求权力并导致人类失去权力。作者旨在检验这一论证链条的逻辑基础是否牢固。

Method: 作者通过逻辑分析，梳理了"从权力寻求到生存风险"的论证结构，指出其依赖于强版本的"工具性收敛论题"；然后逐一考察和批判了该论题的主要哲学辩护，评估其是否足以支撑该强版本主张。

Result: 作者发现，现有的对工具性收敛论题的辩护均未能建立足够强的版本，因此从权力寻求出发论证AI生存风险的路径缺乏坚实前提。

Conclusion: 该结论对长期主义伦理学、AI治理策略以及AI风险研究方法论均有重要启示：这些领域若基于弱版本的工具性收敛论题进行推理，其主张的合理性和紧迫性需要重新审视。

Abstract: Recent years have seen increasing concern that artificial intelligence may soon pose an existential risk to humanity. One leading ground for concern is that artificial agents may be power-seeking, aiming to acquire power and in the process disempowering humanity. I show how the argument from power-seeking rests on a strong version of a claim known as the instrumental convergence thesis. I explore leading defenses of the instrumental convergence thesis and argue that none establishes the thesis in a strong enough form to ground the argument from power-seeking. I discuss implications for longtermism, the governance of artificial intelligence, and the methodology of studying risks posed by artificial agents.

</details>


### [170] [Beyond Pass Rate: A Multilingual, Execution-Grounded Evaluation of Open Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08840)
*Sayed Erfan Arefin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对9个开源代码LLM在12种编程语言的2,707道LeetCode题目上进行了大规模执行评估（共325,343个任务），发现最佳模型(Yi-Coder-9B-Chat)的正确率仅为23.64%，远低于人类57.2%的基准；同时揭示了排名随问题难度、语言和评估指标而变化的切片依赖性，以及编译错误（占63.25%）是主要的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成评估通常依赖单一语言或单一指标的排行榜，无法揭示模型在不同编程语言、问题类型和失败模式下的性能差异，因此需要大规模、多语言、执行驱动的评估来揭示被隐藏的权衡关系。

Method: 选取9个开源代码专用LLM，在12种编程语言的2,707道免费LeetCode题目上进行评估，构建了325,343个问题-模型-语言作业，每个作业关联提示元数据、提取代码、LeetCode执行结果和静态分析信号。

Result: 最佳模型Yi-Coder-9B-Chat平均正确率23.64%，远低于人类57.2%的基准；排名高度依赖评估切片（问题难度、语言等）；编译错误占非通过最优提交的63.25%；静态质量与功能正确性存在分歧。

Conclusion: 多语言、保留工件的评估能够揭示单语言或单指标排行榜所隐藏的模型性能权衡，表明当前开源模型在多语言编程任务上仍有重大差距。

Abstract: Code generation models are typically compared using compact execution benchmarks and aggregate pass rates, but such summaries obscure how performance varies across programming languages, problem families, and failure modes. We present a large-scale, execution-grounded evaluation of 9 openly accessible LLMs specialized for coding on 2,707 free LeetCode problems across 12 programming languages. Our corpus contains 325,343 problem-model-language jobs, each linked to prompt metadata, extracted code, LeetCode execution outcomes, and static-analysis signals. The results show that current open models remain far from the human acceptance reference: the best model, Yi-Coder-9B-Chat, reaches 23.64% mean correctness, compared with a 57.2% human acceptance baseline. Rankings are also slice-dependent: Qwen2.5-Coder-14B-Instruct is strongest on hard problems and distinct-problem coverage, while Gemma-2-27B-IT achieves the highest all-language lint pass rate. Failure analysis shows that compile errors account for 63.25% of non-accepted best submissions, indicating that many failures occur before semantic correctness can be tested. Static quality further diverges from functional correctness. Together, these findings show that multilingual, artifact-preserving evaluation reveals tradeoffs hidden by single-language or single-metric leaderboards.

</details>


### [171] [ZIPP:Zero-shot Image Personalization from Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08841)
*Harini SI,Somesh Singh,Yaman Kumar Singla,David Doermann,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZIPP是一种零样本图像个性化方法，利用自然语言角色描述（persona）通过LLM重写提示词，无需用户数据或微调即可生成符合个人审美的图像。在ZIPBench等基准测试中，该方法在性能上超越微调基线，降低偏好分布差异和子群体偏见，人评胜率达79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型输出偏向大众审美，无法满足用户个性化的、情境依赖的审美偏好。已有方法依赖密集交互历史或逐用户微调，在冷启动场景中失效，且无法处理上下文相关的偏好。

Method: 1) 使用LLM从角色描述（persona）视角重写提示词，引导扩散模型生成个性化输出；2) 在2200万用户的Reddit交互图上训练归纳式图注意力网络，通过双对比目标对齐图结构与视觉行为，学习角色表示；3) 经由多模态大语言模型将学到的表示转化为自然语言角色描述。

Result: 在四个基准和14个LLM上，角色条件化生成一致提升13-20%；零样本设置下匹配或超越基于100+样本微调的基线；偏好分布散度最低（CMMD 0.16 vs 0.55）；显著降低子群体偏见；人评胜率79%（对比通用生成）和58-65%（对比微调基线）。

Conclusion: ZIPP证明了无用户数据与微调即可实现高质量图像个性化，通过自然语言角色描述有效捕捉多元审美偏好，在冷启动场景和偏好多样性上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models are increasingly deployed in open-ended creative contexts, yet their outputs remain impersonal, optimized for aggregate aesthetics rather than individual taste. Human preferences are pluralistic: one user favoring muted, nostalgic portraits may prefer vibrant street photography, while another gravitates toward dreamy film aesthetics. Existing methods require dense interaction histories or per-user fine-tuning, failing in cold-start settings and collapsing context-dependent preferences into a static representation. We introduce zero-shot image personalization from personas (ZIPP), which conditions image generation on natural-language personas (concise descriptors of a user's identity and aesthetic sensibilities) without any user-specific data or weight updates. ZIPP uses an LLM to rewrite prompts from the perspective of a given persona, steering diffusion models toward personalized outputs. To mine personas at scale, we train an inductive Graph Attention Network over a 22M-user Reddit interaction graph with dual contrastive objectives aligning graph structure with visual behavior, then verbalize learned representations into natural-language personas via an MLLM. We introduce ZIPBench, the first zero-shot personalization benchmark with 1.5K users, graph-mined personas, and 40K generated images. Across four benchmarks and 14 LLMs spanning five model families, persona conditioning yields consistent gains (13-20%), with frontier models benefiting most. In the few-shot setting, ZIPP matches or exceeds fine-tuned baselines trained on 100+ examples per user. ZIPP achieves the lowest preference distributional divergence (CMMD 0.16 vs. 0.55), and IPF-normalized demographic evaluation shows it substantially reduces subpopulation bias present in existing methods. Human evaluation confirms a 79% win rate over generic generation and 58-65% over all fine-tuned baselines.

</details>


### [172] [A Resilience-as-a-Service assessment framework for coordinated disruption response in interdependent urban transit systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08849)
*Sara Jaber,S. M. Hassan Mahdavi,Neila Bhouri,Mostafa Ameli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个KPI驱动的、时间索引的框架，结合优化模型与基于智能体的行为模拟，从多维度（脆弱性、适应性、鲁棒性、韧性损失、响应性、成本、排放、公平性）评估城市公共交通中断恢复方案。通过巴黎RER B线案例验证，发现协调多模式策略在服务连续性、总成本、公平性和环境表现上最为均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市公共交通中断需要快速响应策略，但现有研究缺乏一个通用决策支持框架，能够使用一套动态的、面向乘客、运营者和环境的指标来比较不同的中断恢复方案。

Method: 提出KPI驱动的、时间索引的韧性评估框架，结合优化模型与基于智能体的行为模拟，评估辅助线路因抽调车辆支持中断线路而产生的次级服务退化，从脆弱性、适应性、鲁棒性、韧性损失、响应性、成本、排放和公平性等多个互补维度进行评估。

Result: 在巴黎RER B线案例中，协调多模式策略提供了最均衡的韧性表现：高服务连续性、更低的总中断成本，同时改善了公平性并保持了竞争力的环境表现。敏感性分析识别了协调多模式响应最有价值的特定中断条件。

Conclusion: 协调多模式策略优于单一模式替代方案，所提出的框架为决策者提供了比较中断恢复方案的多维度韧性评估工具。

Abstract: Urban public transport disruptions require rapid response strategies, yet existing studies rarely provide a decision support framework to compare alternative disruption response solutions using a common set of dynamic, passenger, operator, and environment oriented indicators. This paper proposes a KPI-driven, time-indexed framework to assess the resilience of disruption response solutions in urban transit systems. The framework combines an optimization model with a behavioral evaluation in agent-based simulation. It also underlays the secondary service degradation induced on helper lines when in-service vehicles are withdrawn to support the disrupted corridor. Rather than treating resilience as a single score, it evaluates complementary dimensions including vulnerability, adaptability, robustness, resilience loss, responsiveness, cost-based performance, emissions, and equity. The framework is implemented for the RER B transit line in the Ile-de-France (Paris) network. Results show that the coordinated strategy provides the most balanced resilience profile, combining high service continuity with lower total disruption cost than single mode alternatives, while also improving equity and maintaining competitive environmental performance. Sensitivity analysis further identifies the disruption conditions under which coordinated multimodal response is most valuable.

</details>


### [173] [Hybrid E-Assessment in Higher Education: Semi-Automated Grading of Paper-Based Written Examinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08855)
*Hartwig Grabowski,Michael Canz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出混合电子评估方法，保留纸质问题导向考试任务并支持半自动评分，利用视觉大语言模型与两遍验证原则识别手写字符，以提高总结性评估的有效性、公平性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全数字化和部分数字化电子评估方法在高等教育总结性考试中存在局限性，包括封闭式问题格式导致的教学法窄化，以及大规模学生群体中出现的组织、技术和法律约束。

Method: 提出混合电子评估方法：保留纸质问题导向考试任务，学生手写填写结构化答案格式，从表格字段中捕获答案；利用视觉大语言模型结合两遍验证原则，与标准答案进行比对以降低误分类。

Result: 视觉大语言模型结合两遍验证原则和标准答案比对，可以减少手写字符识别错误，提升评估的有效性、公平性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 混合电子评估方法能够在保留问题导向考试优势的同时实现半自动评分，为解决大规模总结性评估中的关键瓶颈提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper examines the limitations of fully digital and partially digital e-assessment approaches in summative examinations in higher education. The analysis focuses on the didactic narrowing caused by closed question formats and on organizational, technical, and legal constraints that become particularly relevant in large student cohorts. As an alternative, the paper proposes a hybrid e-assessment approach that retains paper-based, problem-oriented examination tasks while enabling semi-automated grading. Assessment-relevant intermediate results are encoded in a structured answer format, entered by students by hand, and subsequently captured from table fields. The central technical bottleneck is reliable recognition of handwritten characters under realistic examination conditions. Recent vision-capable large language models, combined with a two-pass validation principle and comparison against a solution key, can reduce misclassifications and thereby improve the validity, fairness, and scalability of summative assessment.

</details>


### [174] [Can the Environment Speak for Itself? $T^{2}$-GRPO: A Turn-Trajectory Group Relative Policy Optimization for Caregiver Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08875)
*Yutong Song,Jiang Wu,Pengfei Zhang,Wenjun Huang,Honghui Xu,Nikil Dutt,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出T²-GRPO框架，通过解耦护理强化学习为两个归一化奖励视野（回合级和轨迹级），并采用二元硬否决机制保障安全，在痴呆症护理场景中有效平衡即时患者反馈、长期护理结果和安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野护理智能体在痴呆症护理中难以平衡延迟任务目标与即时环境动态（如患者痛苦和抗拒），轨迹级奖励过于稀疏无法进行回合级信用分配，而外部LLM评估器成本高且难以解读碎片化患者反应。

Method: 提出Turn-Trajectory Group Relative Policy Optimization (T²-GRPO)，将护理RL解耦为两个归一化奖励视野：从冻结的痴呆症患者模拟器环境状态转换中提取密集的回合级奖励（测量患者痛苦和抗拒变化），结合轨迹级评估，通过独立中心化排名归一化保留异质奖励信号并缓解奖励崩溃，同时引入二元硬否决机制保障安全。

Result: 在痴呆症护理场景的广泛实验中，T²-GRPO优于竞争基线，在情感敏感的护理场景中实现了显著改进。

Conclusion: T²-GRPO能有效处理即时患者反馈、长期护理结果和安全约束，为痴呆症护理等情感敏感的长视野任务提供了有效的强化学习框架。

Abstract: Optimizing large language models (LLMs) for long-horizon caregiver agents requires balancing delayed task objectives with immediate environment dynamics, such as patient distress and resistance. In dementia care, this balance is especially difficult: trajectory level rewards are too sparse for turn level credit assignment, while external LLM-based evaluators are costly and can misread fragmented or indirect patient responses. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{T}urn-\textbf{T}rajectory \textbf{G}roup \textbf{R}elative \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{T$^{2}$-GRPO}), a framework that decouples caregiver RL into two normalized reward horizons and enforces safety through a binary hard veto. $T^2$-GRPO derives dense turn-level rewards directly from environment state transitions, measuring changes in patient distress and resistance from a frozen dementia patient simulator. These environment-grounded rewards are combined with trajectory-level evaluations through independent centered-rank normalization, which preserves heterogeneous reward signals and mitigates reward collapse. Extensive experiments on dementia caregivers show that T $^{2}$-GRPO outperforms competitive baselines, indicating a substantial improvement for emotionally sensitive caregiver scenarios that effectively handles immediate patient feedback, long-term care outcomes, and safety constraints.

</details>


### [175] [FAME: Forecastability-Aware Mixture of Experts for Heterogeneous Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08896)
*Qianyang Li,Xingjun Zhang,Shaoxun Wang,Tao Peng,Jia Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FAME框架，通过可预报性指纹和稀疏专家路由，为每个时间序列动态选择最合适的预测模型，在工业级自动售货机数据集上MSE降低12.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模零售与工业预测系统中包含大量异构时间序列，单一模型难以在所有数据场景下表现良好，而密集集成则增加推理成本且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提出FAME框架：1) 用多维可预报性指纹表示每个序列的数据特征；2) 从验证性能中挖掘专家适用性标签；3) 训练成本感知的稀疏路由器，为每个序列激活少量专家（如Top-2）。

Result: 在SNBC 5000+机器、6000万+交易数据集上，FAME Top-2相比最强单一专家LightGBM降低MSE 12.4%，平均每序列仅激活1.92个专家。

Conclusion: 将异构销售预测从启发式模型选择转化为可预报性模式与专家专门化的数据挖掘问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale retail and industrial forecasting systems contain many heterogeneous time series whose lifecycle, sparsity, volatility, seasonality, spectral patterns, and contextual sensitivity differ substantially. A single forecasting model rarely performs well across all regimes, while dense ensembles increase inference cost and provide limited insight into expert suitability. This paper studies forecastability-aware expert routing: learning how data characteristics determine the suitability of forecasting experts. We propose \method{}, a sparse mixture-of-experts framework that represents each series with a multidimensional forecastability fingerprint, mines expert-suitability targets from validation performance, and trains a cost-aware sparse router to activate a small budgeted set of experts for each series. Using a production-scale vending-machine sales dataset from Shandong New Beiyang (SNBC), where the forecasting component has been integrated into the replenishment-planning pipeline, together with public retail benchmarks, we show that expert suitability varies systematically across data regimes. On the industrial dataset with 5,000+ machines and 60M+ transactions, \method{} Top-2 reduces MSE by 12.4\% over the strongest single expert, LightGBM, while executing 1.92 experts per series on average. The deployed component produces demand forecasts, while inventory-oriented gains are estimated by an offline replay simulator under a fixed replenishment policy rather than by online intervention. The framework turns heterogeneous sales forecasting from heuristic model selection into data mining of forecastability patterns and expert specialization. Code is available at https://github.com/hit636/FAME

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Large-time behavior and accuracy of the Mean-Field Ensemble Kalman Filter in the Linear Detectable Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07729)
*Franca Hoffmann,Sangmin Park,Andrew M. Stuart*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性设定下平均场集合卡尔曼滤波（MFEnKF）的理论性质，证明其通过变分近似与最优输运度量可收缩到高斯测度子空间，并在连续时间下与最优滤波器在几乎每个观测路径上一致收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EnKF虽广泛应用，但其相对于最优贝叶斯滤波器的理论理解尚不成熟，本文旨在填补这一空白，在线性设定下建立MFEnKF的严格理论分析。

Method: （1）通过协方差加权的最优输运度量，将MFEnKF推导为贝叶斯更新的变分近似；（2）利用该度量证明MFEnKF向高斯测度子空间的严格收缩性，给出离散与连续时间下的显式几何速率；（3）在连续时间下证明MFEnKF与最优滤波器在几乎每个观测路径上的几乎必然一致性。

Result: （1）MFEnKF可解释为变分贝叶斯更新；（2）MFEnKF以显式几何速率收缩到高斯测度子空间；（3）连续时间下，对任意非退化初始数据，MFEnKF与最优滤波器的均匀连续矩在长时间下几乎必然一致。

Conclusion: 在线性且信号噪声非退化、信号-观测对可检测的假设下，MFEnKF具有严格的理论保证，与最优滤波器在长时间下几乎处处收敛，验证了其作为鲁棒替代方案的合理性。

Abstract: The ensemble Kalman filter (EnKF), originally developed in the geophysical sciences, is now widely used for state and parameter estimation problems in various domains of application. It may be viewed as a robust, cheap-to-implement, alternative to the optimal, Bayesian, filter. However, despite its empirical successes, theoretical understanding of its properties, in relation to the optimal filter, is in its infancy. In this paper we contribute novel theoretical understanding, studying the behavior of the mean-field limit of the ensemble Kalman filter (MFEnKF) in the linear setting. In this setting the MFEnKF coincides with the Kalman filter itself, for Gaussian initial data. We study the MFEnKF with general, non-Gaussian, initial data. Under the assumptions of nondegeneracy of the signal noise and detectability of the signal-observation pair we make three specific contributions. First, we derive the MFEnKF via a variational approximation of the Bayes update using a covariance-weighted optimal transport metric. Secondly, we use this metric to show that the MFEnKF is a strict contraction towards the subspace of Gaussian measures, giving explicit geometric rates in terms of the covariances, in both discrete and continuous time. Finally, in the continuous-time setting, we deduce a stable form of almost sure accuracy of the MFEnKF: given any pair of (nondegenerate) initial data for the MFEnKF and the optimal filter, any uniformly continuous moments of the two filtering distributions coincide in large time, for almost every observation path.

</details>


### [177] [Non-Archimedean Polydisc Spaces and Applications to Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07782)
*Paul Lezeau,Yiannis Fam,Anthea Monod,Yue Ren*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Berkovich几何的非阿基米德空间优化框架，引入多圆盘空间，研究其度量几何性质，提出一类实值函数并证明其通用逼近性质，建立了优化理论并提供了开源Julia实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非阿基米德空间虽然具有严格的层次结构，但缺乏许多理想的几何特征，难以直接应用于优化问题。作者希望构建一个既能保留非阿基米德场的层次结构，又具备良好几何性质的空间，以便将经典优化技术推广到非阿基米德领域。

Method: 1) 定义多圆盘空间（polydisc spaces）作为非阿基米德场上闭球的乘积；2) 研究度量几何性质，证明测地线唯一性等；3) 提出由多项式绝对值线性组合构成的实值函数类，证明其沿测地线分段多项式性质和通用逼近性质；4) 建立优化理论，证明极小值存在性并探索算法；5) 提供开源Julia库实现核心对象和优化过程。

Result: 1) 证明度量树可以自然地嵌入多圆盘空间，表明其表示层次数据的能力；2) 建立了测地线唯一性等度量几何性质；3) 提出的函数类满足通用逼近性质；4) 证明了优化的极小值存在性；5) 提供了开源Julia实现。

Conclusion: 多圆盘空间成功地将非阿基米德场的层次结构与良好的几何性质结合起来，为在非阿基米德空间上进行经典优化提供了理论基础和可用的计算工具。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for optimisation over non-Archimedean spaces inspired by Berkovich geometry. Specifically, we introduce polydisc spaces, which consists of products of closed balls over a non-Archimedean field. These spaces retain the rigid hierarchical structure of the non-Archimedean field whilst acquiring many desirable geometric features absent from it. We show that metric trees embed naturally into these spaces, demonstrating their capacity to represent hierarchical data. We study their metric geometry, establishing properties such as geodesic uniqueness, confirming their comaptibility with classical optimisation techniques. We further propose a class of real-valued functions given by linear combinations of absolute values of polynomials. These functions admit a piecewise polynomial description along geodesics and satisfy a universal approximation property. We formulate a theory of optimisation on polydisc spaces: we prove existence of minimisers and explore algorithms for finding them. We provide an accompanying open-source Julia library implementing the core objects and optimisation procedures introduced.

</details>


### [178] [On the sharp linear convergence rate of the circumcentered--reflection method on subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07888)
*Yunier Bello-Cruz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明，当从子空间V初始化时，圆周反射法（CRM）的收敛率是严格小于Friedrichs角余弦的ρ_V，该率通过V上的自伴算子用Kantorovich不等式得到，且与松弛交替投影族的最优率相同，而记忆型Chebyshev半迭代法可进一步超越该率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRM已知以Friedrichs角的余弦c_F线性收敛，但能否在特定初始化下获得更优的收敛率尚不清楚。本文旨在严格证明CRM在从V初始化时的精确收敛率，并探索其最优性与局限性。

Method: 利用Kantorovich不等式，将CRM在V上的限制转化为单个自伴算子的分析，得到收缩率ρ_V；通过主角理论建立与Gubin-Polyak-Raik框架中线搜索映射A_T和B_T的点态等价性；比较ρ_V与c_F²的关系，并分析过反射和Chebyshev半迭代法的影响。

Result: CRM从V初始化时以ρ_V=(sin²θ_p-sin²θ_F)/(sin²θ_p+sin²θ_F)收缩，该率严格小于c_F（当θ_F<π/2时），且在V中显式射线上可达；ρ_V与松弛交替投影族及自适应线搜索的最优率一致；过反射无法加速；Chebyshev半迭代法可取得严格更小的率，最多优于ρ_V两倍。

Conclusion: CRM在从V初始化时达到了参数无关单步迭代的最优收敛率ρ_V，但若要普遍超越该率，必须引入记忆（如Chebyshev半迭代法）。

Abstract: For two subspaces $U,V\subseteq\RR^n$, the circumcentered--reflection method (CRM) of Behling, Bello-Cruz, and Santos~\cite{BBS2018} computes the projection onto $U\cap V$ using only the reflections across $U$ and $V$, with known linear-convergence rate $c_F$, the cosine of the Friedrichs angle. We prove that, when CRM is initialized in $V$, it contracts at the strictly smaller rate $ρ_V=(\sin^2θ_p-\sin^2θ_F)/(\sin^2θ_p+\sin^2θ_F)$, where $θ_F\in(0,π/2]$ is the Friedrichs angle and $θ_p\in[θ_F,π/2]$ the largest principal angle between $U$ and $V$. The bound is sharp, attained on an explicit ray in $V$, and optimal among parameter-free single-step iterations. The constant itself is not new: Bauschke, Bello-Cruz, Nghia, Phan, and Wang~\cite{BBNPW2016} identified it as the optimal rate of the relaxed alternating-projection family and of their adaptive linesearch map $B_T$. Our contribution is that the parameter-free geometric circumcenter attains it as well, via Kantorovich's inequality applied to a single self-adjoint operator on $V$. Restricted to $V$, CRM coincides pointwise with the linesearch maps $A_T$ and $B_T$ from the Gubin--Polyak--Raik framework~\cite{GPR1967}. We further prove $ρ_V<c_F^2$ whenever $θ_F<π/2$, with one-step convergence exactly when $θ_F=θ_p$. Over-reflecting either or both of $R_U$, $R_V$ inside the circumcenter does not help. Going faster than $ρ_V$ universally requires memory: Chebyshev semi-iteration applied to $P_VP_U$ attains a strictly smaller rate, beating $ρ_V$ by a factor at most $2$, attained in the limit $θ_F\toθ_p$.

</details>


### [179] [Verification Framework for the Union of Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07892)
*Chuanrui Jiang,Andrew Clark*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的切换控制策略框架（union-CBFs），将复杂非凸安全区域表示为多个可计算验证集合的并集，并给出确保有限次切换和正向不变性的充分条件，同时提出两类切换策略及相应的SOS验证算法。实验表明该方法相比高次多项式CBF能获得更大的安全区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CBF方法通常要求安全区域为凸集或单一集合，难以处理复杂的非凸安全区域。本文旨在通过将非凸安全区域分解为多个易于验证的集合的并集，并设计切换控制策略，从而扩大可验证的安全区域范围。

Method: 1) 提出union-CBFs框架，将非凸安全区域表示为多个集合的并集，并设计切换CBF-QP控制器；2) 给出确保有限次切换（Zeno行为避免）和分段正向不变性的充分条件；3) 针对两种切换策略分别提出对应的union-CBF条件；4) 设计Sum-of-Squares（SOS）算法来验证所提条件。

Result: 实验表明，union-CBFs框架相比高次多项式CBF能够获得更大的安全区域；同时SOS验证算法在多项式系统模型上表现出较高的计算效率。

Conclusion: union-CBFs通过集合的并集表示和切换控制策略，有效扩展了CBF方法处理非凸安全区域的能力，并在理论和算法层面提供了可验证的保障。

Abstract: Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) have been proposed to ensure safety of autonomous systems. This paper considers control policies that switch between CBF constraints. Under this approach, we represent a complex non-convex safe region as a union of sets that are computationally tractable to verify. We denote this framework as union-CBFs and make the following contributions. First, considering switching CBF-QP controllers, we propose a sufficient condition that ensures (i) the system undergoes a finite number of switches in any finite time interval and ensures (ii) the forward invariance of the closed-loop system in between switches. Second, we consider two types of switching strategies and propose union-CBFs conditions for each strategy to satisfy (i) and (ii). Third, we formulate Sum-of-Squares (SOS) algorithms to verify the conditions. The experiments show that our union-CBFs framework results in a larger safe region compared to high-degree polynomial CBFs. We also show the efficiency of the verification algorithms using a polynomial system model.

</details>


### [180] [Data-Driven Robust Model Reference Adaptive Control with Parameter Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07911)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Henk J. van Waarde*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的MRAC设计方法，在存在过程噪声时保证自适应增益收敛到匹配方程的近似解，无需持续激励信号，且能明确刻画噪声与匹配误差的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MRAC在噪声下会产生参数漂移，缺乏鲁棒性；现有鲁棒方法又依赖持续激励条件才能保证参数收敛。需要一种无需持续激励、对噪声鲁棒且能保证参数收敛的方法。

Method: 提出数据驱动的MRAC框架，不依赖持续激励信号，利用数据驱动设计保证自适应增益收敛到匹配方程的近似解，并显式刻画噪声与匹配误差的函数关系。

Result: 建立了噪声特性下闭环系统矩阵为Hurwitz的充要条件；无噪声时实现精确参数收敛；对数据条件的要求弱于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在噪声环境下实现了鲁棒的参数收敛，且无需持续激励，对数据条件要求更宽松，具有更强的实用性和理论保证。

Abstract: This paper provides a data-driven design guaranteeing parameter convergence in model reference adaptive control (MRAC) when the to-be-controlled system is subject to process noise. In the context of MRAC, parameter convergence refers to ensuring convergence of the adaptive gains to a solution of the matching equations, or to an approximate solution when noise is present. In classical MRAC, even small noise may induce parameter drift, thus lacking robustness to noise. Meanwhile, existing robust MRAC methods cannot ensure parameter convergence without imposing excitation conditions on data. A key feature of the proposed framework is to ensure convergence of the adaptive gains to an approximate solution of the matching equations without relying on persistently exciting signals. Furthermore, the matching error can be explicitly characterized as a function of the noise. This explicit characterization allows to establish a necessary and sufficient condition on the noise characteristics under which the limit closed-loop system matrix is Hurwitz. In the noise-free case, the proposed framework results in exact parameter convergence. Notably, as compared to existing methods achieving exact parameter convergence in the noise-free case, the condition on data in the proposed framework is weaker.

</details>


### [181] [Combining Reinforcement Learning with Arc-search Interior-Point Method for Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07920)
*Yaguang Yang,Qiang Le,Isaac E. Weintraub*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将强化学习与弧搜索内点法相结合的路径规划框架，通过融合机器学习（实时决策能力）与最优控制（优化性能）的优势，克服各自局限性，数值仿真验证了该方法能有效提升路径规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习方法无需高保真模型、能实时生成可行路径但结果非最优；最优控制/优化方法依赖于高保真模型、计算量大但能产生最优解。为克服各自局限并利用各自优势，提出结合两者的路径规划框架。

Method: 提出一种将强化学习与弧搜索内点法相结合的混合框架用于路径规划。

Result: 数值仿真表明，所提方法有效整合了强化学习的实时决策能力与弧搜索内点法的优化性能，实现了改进的路径规划效果。

Conclusion: 该混合框架成功平衡了实时性与最优性两种需求，为复杂环境下的路径规划提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Path planning in environments containing obstacles has numerous practical applications. The problem is challenging because it is inherently nonlinear and nonconvex. Consequently, a variety of techniques have been developed to address this problem, among which machine learning and optimal control (or optimization) have emerged as two prominent approaches. In general, machine learning methods do not require a high-fidelity model, and a trained agent can often generate a feasible path in real time. However, the resulting path is not necessarily optimal with respect to performance objectives such as minimizing path length or travel time. In contrast, optimal control and optimization methods typically rely on high-fidelity models and often require computational effort that may not satisfy real-time constraints. Nevertheless, these methods are more likely to produce optimal or near-optimal solutions. To overcome the limitations of each approach while exploiting their respective strengths, this paper proposes a framework that combines reinforcement learning with an arc-search interior-point method for path planning. Numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed approach effectively integrates the real-time decision-making capability of reinforcement learning with the optimization performance of the arc-search interior-point method, resulting in improved path-planning performance.

</details>


### [182] [A null controllability data assimilation for the bulk-surface heat equation with dynamic boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07942)
*Javier Ramírez-Ganga*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带Wentzell型动态边界条件的抛物方程，提出了从子域内部观测数据反演最终时刻状态的完整方法，包含Carleman观测不等式、Tikhonov正则化最优控制重构、Lie分裂时空离散、自适应后处理步骤，并在二维Wentzell热方程和Allen-Cahn半线性扩展上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带Wentzell动态边界条件的抛物方程中，从子域内部观测数据反演最终时刻状态的逆问题，该问题在数据同化中具有重要应用。

Method: (i) 建立体-面系统的Carleman型观测不等式；(ii) 将数据同化问题转化为Tikhonov正则化最优控制问题；(iii) 基于Lie分裂的时空离散化，解耦体相和表面动力学；(iv) 提出自适应后处理步骤，无需先验噪声水平即可在完全后处理与原始重构之间自动选择。

Result: 证明了连续问题的精确重构定理，推导了稳定性不等式，分析了惩罚离散格式。二维Wentzell热方程的数值实验验证了方法有效性。对于Allen-Cahn非线性扩展，通过Picard外循环和Schauder不动点收敛性分析，揭示了体-面系统中观测性与非线性之间的固有权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够有效解决带Wentzell边界条件的抛物方程反问题，自适应后处理步骤在噪声环境下表现良好，非线性扩展揭示了体-面耦合系统中观测性与非线性的权衡关系。

Abstract: We address the inverse problem of reconstructing the state at a final time $T_0$ of a parabolic equation with dynamic boundary conditions of Wentzell type, from a distributed interior observation on a subdomain $ω\subset Ω$. Our approach combines (i) a Carleman-type observability inequality for the bulk-surface system, with (ii) a Tikhonov-regularized optimal-control reformulation of the data assimilation problem, and (iii) a fully implementable spatio-temporal discretization based on a Lie splitting that decouples the bulk and surface dynamics. We prove an exact reconstruction theorem for the continuous problem, derive a stability inequality, and analyze a penalized discrete scheme. We then propose an adaptive post-processing step that optimally combines the regularized reconstruction with the observation itself; under noisy data, the resulting estimator automatically selects between full post-processing and the raw reconstruction without prior knowledge of the noise level. Extensive numerical experiments on a two-dimensional Wentzell heat equation validate the method. A semilinear extension to the Allen-Cahn nonlinearity is treated via a Picard outer loop with Schauder fixed-point convergence; two complementary experiments reveal an intrinsic observability-nonlinearity trade-off in the bulk-surface setting.

</details>


### [183] [An Iterative Computational Framework for Infinite-Horizon Mean-Field Linear-Quadratic Zero-Sum Stochastic Differential Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08145)
*Yiyuan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代计算框架，用于求解无限时域两人平均场线性二次零和随机微分博弈的鞍点解，通过构造单调递增矩阵序列将强耦合的原始问题解耦为可解子问题，并建立了严格的收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法受限于特定的简化设置，缺乏适用于广泛博弈驱动Riccati方程的统一计算方法。因此，需要开发一种通用计算范式来求解此类复杂问题。

Method: 通过推广经典迭代框架，构造单调递增矩阵序列，将强耦合的原始问题解耦为一系列可处理的子问题；然后顺序计算这些子问题的耦合代数Riccati方程的镇定解，进而推导出原始问题的解。

Result: 建立了严格的收敛性分析，验证了所提迭代算法的有效性，并提出了第一个适用于广泛博弈驱动Riccati方程的通用计算范式。

Conclusion: 该框架突破了现有算法的局限性，为求解一类广泛的博弈驱动Riccati方程提供了首个通用计算方法。

Abstract: This work develops an iterative computational framework to obtain saddle-point solutions for infinite-horizon two-person mean-field linear-quadratic zero-sum stochastic differential games. By generalizing classical iterative framework, we construct a monotonically increasing matrix sequence to decouple the strongly coupled, analytically intractable original problem into a set of tractable subproblems. By sequentially computing the stabilizing solutions of the coupled algebraic Riccati equations for these subproblems, we can further derive the solution to the original problem. Rigorous convergence analysis is established to validate the proposed iterative algorithm. Different from existing algorithms limited to specific simplified setups, this framework proposes the first universal computational paradigm applicable to a broad class of game-driven Riccati equations.

</details>


### [184] [Latent Structural Categorical Matrix Completion with Application to Quasispecies Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08188)
*Qian Zhang,Meixia Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: LCMC提出了一种基于二元张量表示的双循环优化框架，用于处理分类矩阵补全问题，通过外循环自适应估计潜在维度、内循环进行张量分解，并引入分裂-合并-优化策略和自适应数据缩减技术，在病毒准种重建等任务上取得了更优的精度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵补全方法主要针对实值数据设计，在处理分类变量（特别是无序的离散变量）时存在局限性，缺乏能够保留分类变量非序数性质的专用方法。

Method: 采用二元张量表示，将每个分类条目编码为沿第三张量模态的one-hot向量；设计双循环优化框架，外循环根据内循环反馈自适应估计潜在维度，内循环通过张量分解重构分类矩阵；引入分裂-合并-优化策略和自适应数据缩减技术提升可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在合成数据集和病毒准种重建的真实数据集上，LCMC相比现有方法取得了更优的准确性和效率。

Conclusion: 基于二元张量表示的双循环优化框架能够有效处理分类矩阵补全问题，在保留分类变量离散、非序数性质的同时实现了高精度和高效率，相关理论分析也支撑了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Matrix completion has been extensively studied for real-valued data, but existing methods are often limited in handling categorical variables. We propose LCMC, a double-loop optimization framework for categorical matrix completion via latent factorization based on a binary tensor representation. In this setting, each categorical entry is encoded as a one-hot vector along a third tensor mode, thereby preserving its discrete, non-ordinal nature. The outer loop adaptively estimates the latent dimension by iteratively updating it with feedback from the inner loop, while the inner loop reconstructs the categorical matrix through tensor factorization, supported by a corresponding theoretical analysis.
