To further improve scalability and robustness, we introduce enhancements including a split-merge-refine strategy and an adaptive data reduction technique. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets in viral quasispecies reconstruction, demonstrate that LCMC achieves superior accuracy and efficiency compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [185] [Equivalence between time and norm optimal control problems of stochastic differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08219)
*Yanqing Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了可控随机微分方程最小时间和最小范数控制问题之间的等价性，通过显式构造最小范数控制证明了最小范数函数的连续性，并提出了有效的数值方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机微分方程框架下，由于容许控制的适应性，确定性情形中有效的时间平移技术不再适用，因此需要新的方法建立最小时间与最小范数控制问题的等价性。

Method: 通过显式构造最小范数控制，证明了最小范数函数的连续性，从而建立两者等价性。

Result: 建立了可控随机微分方程中最小时间与最小范数控制问题的等价性，并提出了高效的数值方案。

Conclusion: 该构造性方法不仅证明了理论等价性，还有助于设计最小范数和时间控制的高效数值算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the equivalence between minimal time and minimal norm control problems for controllable stochastic differential equations (SDEs). The core of this equivalence lies in proving the continuity of the minimal norm function. Unlike the deterministic setting, where the time translation technique is effective, this approach is not applicable due to the adapted nature of the admissible controls in SDEs. To address this challenge, we construct the minimal norm control explicitly, thereby achieving the desired continuity. This constructive approach not only proves theoretical equivalence but also facilitates the design of efficient numerical schemes for minimal norm and time controls.

</details>


### [186] [Robust Optimization for Green Ammonia Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08335)
*Karl Zhu,Yassine Bohafid,Omar Kadir,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对绿色氨系统中哈伯-博世工艺最小负荷需求与可再生能源不确定性的矛盾，提出了一个结合k-means聚类与鲁棒优化的场景缩减框架，以及自适应鲁棒滚动时域运行模型，实现了比现有约束聚合方法更可行的容量规划方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绿色氨系统的核心挑战在于如何在太阳能和风能的不确定性下，满足哈伯-博世工艺的最小负荷运行要求。

Method: 开发了包含战略容量规划（混合整数优化，支持热待机和停机两种灵活运行模式）和运行流模型的两阶段鲁棒优化框架；提出基于k-means聚类与鲁棒优化结合的场景缩减方法；构建自适应鲁棒滚动时域模型处理预测不确定性。

Result: 所提框架在样本外仿真中能生成可行的容量计划，而基于约束聚合的现有方法无法满足HB最小负荷要求。自适应策略比静态鲁棒策略在相同鲁棒性水平下能实现更高的氨产量，但对不确定性集外的实现保护较弱。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒优化框架有效解决了绿色氨系统的容量规划与运行问题，在可行性和经济性之间实现了平衡，但自适应策略在极端不确定性下的鲁棒性有限。

Abstract: The central challenge in optimizing green ammonia systems is satisfying the minimum-load requirements of the Haber-Bosch (HB) process under renewable uncertainty. We develop a robust optimization framework consisting of a strategic capacity planning model and an operational flow model under solar and wind uncertainty. The strategic model is a mixed-integer optimization (MIO) problem with flexible HB operating modes, namely hot-idling and shutdowns. To address the resulting computational challenges, we propose a robust scenario-reduction framework that combines k-means clustering with robust optimization to generate adversarial renewable trajectories. For the operational model, we develop adaptive robust rolling-horizon formulations under forecast uncertainty. Computational results show that the proposed framework produces feasible capacity plans under out-of-sample simulation, whereas existing approaches based on constraint aggregation fail to satisfy HB minimum-load requirements. Adaptive policies achieve higher ammonia production than static robust policies for a given robustness level, but provide weaker protection against realizations outside the uncertainty set.

</details>


### [187] [Temperature control by a state-delay nash strategy: theory and experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08344)
*César Adair Rodríguez Castro,Manuel Alejando Jímenez Lizarraga,Jorge Manuel Ortega Martínez,Omar Jacobo Santos Sánchez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将纳什均衡算法扩展到具有状态延迟的确定性LQ型系统的控制方法，通过构造性方法求解策略参数，并利用Bellman方程作为约束条件。算法在ESP32微控制器驱动的热原型上实现，并与两个商用/工业PID控制器中的最优PI控制进行了性能比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展纳什均衡算法至存在状态延迟的系统，填补已有方法在该类系统上的空白，并为具有延迟的线性二次型系统提供最优控制策略。

Method: 采用构造性方法：首先提出策略形式并闭环求Cauchy解；其次根据表达式表示代价函数；最后利用Bellman方程作为约束条件求解策略参数。同时进行了闭环稳定性证明。

Result: 算法在ESP32微控制器驱动的热原型上成功实现，并与商用/工业PID控制器（REX-C100）中的最优PI控制进行了性能对比。

Conclusion: 所述方法能够有效处理状态延迟系统的纳什均衡问题，并在实际热控制原型中表现出优于或可比的性能。

Abstract: This work expands the Nash equilibrium algorithm to those systems that have a state delay within the deterministic case, a Linear-Quadratic (LQ) type performance index with generalized cross terms is used, following the constructive approach on optimal control: first, propose the strategies form, close the loop and find the Cauchy solution; secondly, express the cost function according to those expressions; and finally use the Bellman equation as restrictions to find the strategies parameters. The stability of the closed loop is demonstrated. The algorithm is applied to a thermal prototype with an ESP32 micro-controller and its performance is compared with optimal PI controls inside two commercial/industrial PID controllers REX-C100.

</details>


### [188] [Advancing Branch-and-Price for Graph Coloring: New Pricing Strategies and Benchmark Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08356)
*Mingming Zheng,Roberto Baldacci,Fabio Furini,Qinghua Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出BPCOL+，一种基于分支定价的图着色精确算法，利用零压缩二元决策图（ZDD）改进定价策略，在DIMACS基准测试和Erdős–Rényi实例上优于现有精确算法，与最先进的SAT求解器具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色问题（GCP）是经典的组合优化问题，现有精确算法在求解效率和规模上仍有提升空间，特别是在定价问题的求解上存在瓶颈。

Method: BPCOL+采用基于最大稳定集集合覆盖模型的分支定价框架，利用零压缩二元决策图（ZDD）增强定价策略；在根节点通过启发式过程和列生成计算上下界，并通过利用对偶向量应用最大稳定集约简技术来缩小ZDD规模。

Result: 在137个DIMACS实例中1小时内求解96个；在5000个Erdős–Rényi实例中证明4641个实例的最优性；优于现有精确分支定价算法，与最先进的SAT求解器高度竞争。

Conclusion: BPCOL+通过ZDD增强定价和最大稳定集约简技术，显著提升了图着色问题的精确求解能力，验证了分支定价框架在该问题上的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes BPCOL+, an exact branch-and-price algorithm for the Graph Coloring Problem. The algorithm integrates enhanced pricing strategies based on Zero-Suppressed Binary Decision Diagrams (ZDDs) for solving the pricing problem associated with the maximal-stable-set-based set-covering formulation. After computing upper and lower bounds at the root node using heuristic procedures and column generation, BPCOL+ reduces the size of the ZDD by applying maximal stable set reduction techniques that exploit alternative dual vectors. Computational experiments on the 137 DIMACS benchmark instances and on 5,000 recently proposed Erdős--Rényi instances show that BPCOL+ outperforms existing exact branch-and-price algorithms and remains highly competitive with state-of-the-art SAT-based exact solvers. In particular, BPCOL+ solves 96 DIMACS instances within one hour and proves optimality for 4,641 of the 5,000 Erdős--Rényi instances.

</details>


### [189] [Variational Analysis of Metric Projections onto Isotone Projection Cones via Coderivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08386)
*Le Van Hien*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限维欧氏空间中度量投影映射到保序投影锥的变分性质，推导出了Fréchet和Mordukhovich余导数的显式公式，并应用于计算投影映射的覆盖常数以及建立参数非线性互补问题解映射的Aubin性质的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般锥体（非正交）背景下，度量投影映射的变分分析缺乏成熟的工具。本文旨在填补这一空白，为保序投影锥上的投影算子提供可计算的变分刻画。

Method: 基于参考点邻域内通过关联生成系统对投影映射进行局部描述，进而推导Fréchet余导数和Mordukhovich余导数的显式公式。

Result: 给出了投影映射的余导数显式计算公式，计算了投影映射的覆盖常数，并建立了参数非线性互补问题解映射满足Aubin性质的充分条件。

Conclusion: 本文为一般锥体（无法利用正交性）上的度量投影变分分析提供了新工具，推进了有限维互补系统稳定性理论的发展。

Abstract: In this paper, we study variational properties of the metric projection mapping onto isotone projection cones in finite-dimensional Euclidean spaces. We derive explicit formulas for both the Fréchet coderivative and the Mordukhovich coderivative of the projection operator. The analysis is based on a local description of the projection mapping via an associated generating system in a neighborhood of a reference point, which leads to computable coderivative characterizations. As an application, we compute the covering constant of the projection mapping, providing a quantitative description of its local regularity. Furthermore, we establish verifiable sufficient conditions for the Aubin property of the solution mapping associated with parametric nonlinear complementarity problems associated with isotone projection cones.
  The obtained results contribute to the variational analysis of metric projections in a general cone setting where orthogonality arguments are not available, and to the stability theory of complementarity systems in finite dimensions.

</details>


### [190] [Augmented Lagrangian methods for nonlinear semidefinite programming with complementarity constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08424)
*Daiana O. Santos,Carina M. Costa,Evelin H. M. Krulikovski e Marina Geremia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对带互补约束的非线性半定规划（SDCMPCC）这类高度退化问题，本文引入基于谱分解的重构框架，并在此框架下分析增广拉格朗日方法，证明在适当扩展的Robinson条件下，生成的序列的每个聚点都是W-稳定点，或在更严格的子问题求解条件下为C-稳定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDCMPCC是一类高度退化的问题，经典最优性条件通常失效。虽然弱平稳性条件（如W-平稳性）已被提出以应对退化性，但这些概念在半定互补设置下的算法含义尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出一种基于互补结构谱分解的重构方法，该重构保持局部解并允许可处理的分析。在此框架下，分析增广拉格朗日方法，并证明在Robinson条件的适当扩展下，算法序列的每个聚点满足W-平稳性；在更严格的子问题求解条件下满足C-平稳性。

Result: 证明了在扩展的Robinson条件下，增广拉格朗日方法生成的序列的每个聚点都是W-稳定的；在更严格的子问题求解条件下，聚点达到C-稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了SDCMPCC弱平稳性条件在算法实现层面的空白，为求解这类高度退化问题提供了理论保证和可行算法。

Abstract: We consider nonlinear semidefinite programming problems with complementarity constraints (SDCMPCC), a class of highly degenerate problems where classical optimality conditions typically fail. In this context, weak stationarity conditions have been developed to address their degeneracy. While these notions are well understood, their algorithmic implications remain largely unexplored in semidefinite complementarity settings. We introduce a reformulation based on the spectral decomposition of the complementarity structure, which preserves local solutions and enables a tractable analysis. Within this framework, we analyze an augmented Lagrangian method for SDCMPCC and prove that under a suitable extension of Robinson's condition, every accumulation point of the generated sequence is W-stationary, or C-stationary under a stricter condition for solving the subproblems.

</details>


### [191] [Optimal Harvesting under Stochastic Control: HJB Equation and Feynman-Kac Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08428)
*Paramahansa Pramanik,Fatamatuj Johora*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究在随机微分方程框架下探讨了可再生生物资源的最优收获策略，通过HJB方程和Feynman-Kac表示两种互补方法，为不确定环境下的可持续资源管理提供了理论一致且实践相关的优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续资源管理需要能够兼顾环境变异性和生态不确定性的收获策略，现有方法在随机波动条件下缺乏统一的优化框架和概率解释。

Method: 采用HJB方程（提供动态优化规则和非线性偏微分方程刻画值函数）与Feynman-Kac表示（提供期望收益的概率解释）两种互补方法进行对比分析。

Result: 两种方法在理论上具有一致性，均能为不确定性条件下的经济高效且生态可持续的收获政策提供设计依据。

Conclusion: HJB方法和Feynman-Kac表示在随机资源管理问题上互补且有效，有助于制定兼顾经济效益与生态可持续性的最优收获策略。

Abstract: Sustainable resource management requires harvesting strategies that account for environmental variability and ecological uncertainty. This study investigates optimal harvesting of renewable biological resources within a stochastic framework, where population dynamics are influenced by random environmental fluctuations and modeled using stochastic differential equations. Two complementary approaches are employed: the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation and the Feynman-Kac representation. The HJB framework provides a dynamic optimization rule and characterizes the value function through a nonlinear partial differential equation, while the Feynman-Kac approach offers a probabilistic interpretation of expected returns. A comparative analysis demonstrates the theoretical consistency and practical relevance of both methods for designing economically efficient and ecologically sustainable harvesting policies under uncertainty.

</details>


### [192] [Block coordinate descent for joint delay-energy optimization in multi-hop D2D networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08544)
*Kai-Xiang Hu,Jacek Gondzio,Caixia Kou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种块坐标下降（BCD）框架，联合优化多跳D2D网络中的路由路径、发射功率和带宽分配，以最小化最大传输时间（瓶颈延迟）和总能耗。在网络层提出MF-FW（快速）和LR-PDIPM（高精度）两种自适应路由算法，在物理层设计并行对偶上升算法实现资源分配的全局最优。实验表明LR-PDIPM变体实现最高9.14倍能耗降低，MF-FW变体可在数秒内获得近优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳D2D网络中网络层路由与物理层资源分配紧密耦合，传统基于平均性能的指标难以有效优化网络级指标，亟需联合优化框架以实现延迟与能耗的帕累托最优权衡。

Method: 提出BCD框架，网络层：1) 矩阵自由Frank-Wolfe (MF-FW)算法用于密集拓扑快速执行；2) 低秩原始-对偶内点法 (LR-PDIPM)利用Sherman-Morrison公式避免稠密矩阵求逆实现高精度求解。物理层：设计基于时域视角变换的并行对偶上升算法，实现资源分配子问题的全局最优解。证明BCD框架收敛到驻点的ε-邻域。

Result: LR-PDIPM变体相比最优基线实现最高9.14倍总能耗降低，能量效率提升一个数量级，最大延迟差距控制在3.78倍以内。MF-FW变体通过热启动可在数秒内识别近优解。

Conclusion: 所提出的BCD框架在延迟-能耗权衡上具有显著优势，LR-PDIPM提供高精度解，MF-FW提供高效工程实现方案，两种变体分别适用于不同精度-速度需求场景。

Abstract: In multi-hop device-to-device (D2D) networks, the optimization of network-level metrics is particularly difficult due to the tight coupling between network-layer routing and physical-layer resource allocation. Departing from traditional average-performance metrics, this paper addresses the joint optimization of routing paths, transmission power, and bandwidth allocation. We formulate a generalized cost function to minimize the maximum transmission time (i.e., the bottleneck delay) alongside the total energy consumption. To tackle the resulting highly non-convex formulation, we propose a novel block coordinate descent (BCD) framework. At the network layer, we develop two adaptive routing algorithms: a matrix-free Frank-Wolfe (MF-FW) algorithm for fast execution in dense topologies, and a low-rank primal-dual interior-point method (LR-PDIPM) that bypasses dense matrix inversions via the Sherman-Morrison formula for high-precision solutions. At the physical layer, we design a parallel dual ascent algorithm leveraging a time-domain perspective transformation to solve the resource allocation subproblem to global optimality. The proposed BCD framework is proven to converge to an ε-neighborhood of a stationary point. Through comprehensive experiments, the proposed BCD framework establishes its superiority in achieving the optimal delay-energy trade-off. Specifically, the LR-PDIPM variant achieves a maximum 9.14-fold reduction in total energy consumption and up to an order of magnitude improvement in energy efficiency, while maintaining a bounded maximum delay gap (up to 3.78-fold) relative to the best baseline. Meanwhile, the warm-start MF-FW variant identifies near-optimal solutions in mere seconds, serving as a highly practical engineering approach.

</details>


### [193] [Parameter Tuning with Generalization Guarantees for GPU-Accelerated Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08638)
*Siddharth Prasad,Dravyansh Sharma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对(cu)PDLP这一现代一阶线性规划求解器中的超参数调优问题，推导了泛化保证：首先刻画了PDHG算法中步长和原始权重的线性样本复杂度，然后通过结构分析将PDLP中多种专用技术（预处理、自适应步长、平均、自适应重启、平滑原始权重更新）的行为纳入分析，获得多项式样本复杂度保证，最后通过实验验证了数据驱动调参的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际可并行化的一阶线性规划方法在大规模问题上表现优异，但其性能高度依赖超参数选择，而现有的理论分析缺乏对复杂求解器中超参数调优的泛化保证。

Method: 首先严格刻画PDHG算法关于步长和原始权重的行为，建立线性样本复杂度保证；然后对PDLP进行结构分析，将其核心技术（预处理、自适应步长、平均、自适应重启、平滑原始权重更新）纳入理论框架；利用数据驱动算法设计的最新进展，推导多项式样本复杂度保证。

Result: 获得了PDHG超参数学习的线性样本复杂度保证，以及PDLP完整求解器的多项式样本复杂度保证；实验证明数据驱动调参对PDLP性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: 展示了数据驱动算法设计工具箱在复杂现代优化算法中进行原理性超参数调优的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Recent research has developed practical, parallelizable first-order methods for large scale linear programming, but performance is highly dependent on hyperparameter selection. We derive generalization guarantees for hyperparameter tuning within (cu)PDLP, a state-of-the-art first-order LP solver designed for modern hardware. First, we pin down the behavior of PDHG, the primal-dual hybrid gradient algorithm that underlies PDLP, as a function of its step size and primal weight, leading to linear sample complexity guarantees for learning those parameters. We then conduct a structural analysis of PDLP, which augments PDHG with several specialized techniques like preconditioning, adaptive step sizes, averaging, adaptive restarts, and smoothed primal weight updates. Our analysis captures the behavior of the solution trajectory as a function of the hyperparameters and leverages recent advances in data-driven algorithm design to obtain polynomial sample complexity guarantees for learning those hyperparameters. Finally, we conduct proof-of-concept experiments that demonstrate the need for data-driven PDLP parameter tuning. Our results showcase the versatility of the data-driven algorithm design toolkit for principled hyperparameter tuning within solver-grade implementations of complex modern optimization algorithms.

</details>


### [194] [Explainable Optimization: A Call for Interdisciplinary Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08675)
*Nurşen Aydın,Ş. İlker Birbil,İlker Küçükparlak,Altuğ Yalçıntaş*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文呼吁发展可解释优化（XOpt）作为一个跨学科领域，以解决运筹学与管理科学中优化模型因缺乏行为、认知和实用视角的解释而导致的决策信任与实施障碍问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的优化模型主要关注计算标准（可行性、最优性、可扩展性、求解时间），而将解释视为次要问题。数学透明度（如目标函数、约束条件、影子价格、灵敏度分析）并不能自动提供利益相关者所需的理解、信任、质疑和实施优化决策的论证形式。

Method: 本文以概念性论证的方式，提出将可解释优化（XOpt）建立为一个独立的跨学科研究领域，整合行为科学、认知科学和实用主义视角，超越传统的算法效率导向。

Result: 指出当前优化领域存在“解释赤字”，即数学透明度与利益相关者所需的论证形式之间存在鸿沟，需要系统性地发展可解释优化方法。

Conclusion: 运筹学与管理科学需要发展可解释优化（XOpt）作为跨学科领域，将解释提升为与计算效率同等重要的核心关注点。

Abstract: Operations research and management science models support decisions that affect patients, workers, citizens, and public institutions. Decision-makers, such as clinicians approving surgical schedules, planners allocating disaster relief resources, or managers designing workforce rotations, increasingly require clear and actionable justifications that bridge the gap between mathematical optimization outputs and the intuitive reasoning stakeholders need to trust, contest, and implement recommended decisions. Yet the field has traditionally evaluated optimization models through computational criteria such as feasibility, optimality, scalability, and solution time, while treating explanation as a secondary concern. Mathematical transparency, provided through access to objectives, constraints, shadow prices, or sensitivity reports, does not automatically offer the forms of justification that stakeholders need to understand, trust, contest, or implement optimization-based decisions. This paper calls for the development of explainable optimization (XOpt) as a distinct interdisciplinary area that moves beyond algorithmic efficiency and incorporates behavioral, cognitive, and pragmatic perspectives to address this explanatory deficit.

</details>


### [195] [OptMuon: Closed-Loop Orthogonalized Momentum Methods for Stochastic Optimization with Zero-Noise Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08783)
*Ganzhao Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出OptMuon，一种将Muon风格动量正交化与闭环自适应标量系数调度相结合的自适应方法，在随机非凸优化中实现了噪声自适应收敛率，且无需依赖Lipschitz常数等先验超参数选择，在零噪声下自动退化为近优确定性一阶速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正交化动量方法（如Muon风格优化器）通常使用固定或开环幅度规则，未能根据优化轨迹显式校准更新幅度。受Lipschitz-free和噪声自适应方法中闭环视角的启发，本文旨在将闭环自适应标量调度与动量正交化结合，实现无需人工调参的噪声自适应优化。

Method: OptMuon将Muon风格的极因子方向（polar-factor directions）与轨迹依赖的AdaGrad-Norm类型系数调度相结合。该调度不使用光滑常数、方差水平或有界梯度常数，通过运行最大值修正（running-maximum correction）防止孤立梯度尖峰导致系数过度坍缩。OptMuon包含两个变体：OptMuon-A（平均光滑假设）和OptMuon-I（个体光滑假设）。

Result: OptMuon-A在平均光滑下达到噪声自适应速率\(\tilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/2}+σ^{1/2}T^{-1/4})\)，OptMuon-I在个体光滑下达到\(\tilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/2}+σ^{1/3}T^{-1/3})\)。零噪声下两变体均自动退化为近优确定性一阶速率\(\tilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/2})\)，无需手动超参数重调。

Conclusion: 闭环标量自适应可以与Muon风格动量正交化相结合，在保留噪声自适应性的同时实现零噪声近优性（至多对数因子损失），为大规模深度学习中更新幅度的自适应校准提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Orthogonalized momentum updates, as used in Muon-style optimizers, have recently shown strong empirical stability in large-scale deep learning. However, existing orthogonalized methods are typically paired with constant or open-loop magnitude rules, and therefore do not explicitly calibrate their update magnitudes from the observed optimization trajectory. Motivated by the closed-loop perspective behind Lipschitz-free and noise-adaptive methods, we propose OptMuon, a family of adaptive momentum orthogonalization methods for stochastic nonconvex optimization. OptMuon combines Muon-style polar-factor directions with a trajectory-dependent AdaGrad-Norm-type coefficient schedule, so that the update magnitude is determined by the observed gradient and momentum history rather than by a prescribed Lipschitz-dependent rule. The schedule does not use the smoothness constant, the variance level, or the bounded-gradient constant in parameter selection, and its running-maximum correction prevents isolated gradient spikes from causing excessive coefficient collapse. Under lower-boundedness, unbiased stochastic gradients with bounded variance, smoothness, and an almost-sure bounded stochastic-gradient condition, we prove two complementary guarantees. OptMuon-A achieves the noise-adaptive rate \(\tilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/2}+σ^{1/2}T^{-1/4})\) under average smoothness, while OptMuon-I achieves \(\tilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/2}+σ^{1/3}T^{-1/3})\) under individual smoothness. In the zero-noise regime, both bounds automatically reduce to a nearly optimal deterministic first-order rate \(\tilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/2})\) without manual hyperparameter retuning. These results show that closed-loop scalar adaptation can be combined with Muon-style momentum orthogonalization while retaining noise adaptivity and zero-noise optimality up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [196] [Optimal Control and Dissipativity of Linear Hermitian Matrix-Valued Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08856)
*Corentin Briat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为线性脉冲系统（状态为Hermitian矩阵）建立统一框架，涵盖最优控制、最优转向、耗散性分析和零和微分博弈，基于三个核心工具导出统一的LMI条件与显式最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对连续/离散时间线性系统、切换系统、线性混合系统的控制与博弈问题缺乏统一处理框架，且常依赖时不变或周期性假设。本文旨在为线性脉冲系统提供一个统一的、时变假设下的理论框架。

Method: 提出三个核心工具：(1) 关键恒等式（联系成本、轨迹与对偶变量）；(2) 扩展Schur补引理；(3) Schur内积分解。将这些工具一致地应用于流动积分与每次跳跃，导出统一的充分必要条件、对偶LMI刻画及显式最优策略。

Result: 获得了所有问题类在有限/无限时域、时变假设下结构统一的充分必要条件、对偶LMI刻画和显式最优策略，以及因果驻留时间策略。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效统一了线性脉冲系统中多个控制与博弈问题，为时变假设下的分析与设计提供了系统化的LMI工具。

Abstract: We develop a unified framework for linear-cost optimal control, finite-time optimal steering, dissipativity analysis, and zero-sum differential games for linear impulsive systems whose state is a Hermitian matrix evolving in $\mathbb{H}^{n+m}_{\succeq0}$, a class that encompasses continuous- and discrete-time linear systems and switched systems as degenerate cases, and includes the second-order moment dynamics of linear (stochastic) hybrid systems. The entire theory rests on three tools: a single \emph{key identity} relating cost, trajectory, and a dual variable, an Extended Schur complement lemma, and a Schur inner-product decomposition, applied identically to the flow integral and to each jump. These yield structurally uniform sufficient and necessary conditions, dual linear matrix inequality (LMI) characterizations, and explicit optimal policies for every problem class, on both finite and infinite horizons under time-varying assumptions (without time invariance or periodicity), together with causal dwell-time policies for the problems that admit them.

</details>


### [197] [Not All Warm Starts Help: Benchmarking Primal-Dual Initializations for ACOPF Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08984)
*Babak Taheri,Daniel K. Molzahn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了AC最优潮流问题中不同初始化策略对IPOPT内点法求解性能的影响，基于19个PGLib-OPF算例（5至30,000节点）进行了基准测试。实验表明：完全重启（完整初值+对偶）在基线收敛算例上中位加速比达47.6%，而大多数部分初值+对偶重启反而增加求解时间或降低收敛可靠性。将对偶分解为约束乘子和边界乘子后，发现"覆盖度"而非对偶本身决定鲁棒性：完整边界乘子向量收敛率达90.7%、中位加速+26.8%，而部分覆盖则降至70.4%和-31.1%。DC预求解在实际端到端比较中无统计显著收益（p=0.4171）。对学习型热启动方法的建议：优先预测完整原始向量，若只能部分覆盖则优先电压变量，避免部分或不一致的对偶预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管热启动被广泛用于加速AC最优潮流求解，但不同初始化策略对主导大规模ACOPF算法（原始-对偶内点法）的影响缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 使用IPOPT求解器在19个PGLib-OPF算例（5至30,000节点）上，测试了原始变量块{P_g, Q_g, V_m, V_a}所有15种非空子集在oracle条件下的组合，以及三种DC预求解组合在实际场景下的表现。

Result: 完整重启（O-PD）在基线收敛算例上中位加速47.6%；12/14的部分O-PD组合中位加速为负；完整边界乘子向量收敛率90.7%、中位加速+26.8%，而部分覆盖降至70.4%和-31.1%；DC预求解在端到端比较中无统计显著收益（p=0.4171）。

Conclusion: 对学习型热启动方法的建议排序：优先预测完整原始向量；若只能部分覆盖，优先电压变量；避免部分或不一致的对偶预测，除非原始估计接近完整。

Abstract: Warm starts are widely used to accelerate AC optimal power flow (ACOPF) solves, but the impact of different initialization strategies has received limited systematic study, particularly for the primal-dual interior-point methods that dominate large-scale ACOPF algorithms. This paper benchmarks initialization strategies for ACOPF solved with the interior-point solver IPOPT on 19 PGLib-OPF instances (5 to 30,000 buses), testing all 15 non-empty subsets of the primal blocks $\{P_g, Q_g, V_m, V_a\}$ under oracle conditions and three DC-seeded combinations in a practical setting. The experiments show that most partial primal-plus-dual restarts increase solve time or reduce convergence reliability. Among the oracle primal-plus-dual (O-PD) configurations, only the complete restart reliably converges on every baseline-convergent case, reaching a $47.6\%$ median solve-time speedup. Twelve of the 14 partial O-PD combinations have negative median speedups, and several fail repeatedly on larger networks. Decomposing the dual into constraint and bound multipliers shows that \emph{coverage}, not the presence of duals per se, governs robustness: the full bound-multiplier vector reaches 90.7\% convergence and a $+26.8$\% median speedup, whereas block-matched coverage (oracle multipliers on some bounds, defaults on the rest) drops to 70.4\% and $-31.1$\%. Practical DC seeding sometimes helps the AC solve, but the benefit is no longer statistically significant once the DCOPF presolve cost is included in the end-to-end comparison ($p = 0.4171$). For learned warm-start methods, the results support the following target ordering: predict the full primal vector first; if only partial coverage is possible, prioritize voltage variables; and avoid partial or inconsistent dual predictions unless the primal estimate is nearly complete.

</details>


### [198] [Time-Varying Perturbations of Contractive Systems With an Application to Safe Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09035)
*Andreas Oliveira,Arya Honarpisheh,Mustafa Bozdag,Mario Sznaier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对收缩动力学系统提出了新的时变扰动条件，能够保持扰动系统的增量指数稳定性，并在特定条件下保证渐近收敛到名义动态的解。作为应用，设计了一个安全滤波器，用于将名义收缩系统引导至期望平衡点同时避开不安全区域，并通过实例展示了相比现有方法的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典稳定性分析依赖于平衡点，而收缩系统具有不依赖平衡点的特性且拥有丰富的鲁棒性界。本文旨在克服经典扰动理论中平衡点依赖的局限性，为收缩动力学发展新的时变扰动条件。

Method: 提出了新的时变扰动条件，证明其能保持扰动系统的增量指数稳定性，并在特定条件下保证渐近收敛到名义动力学解。基于此设计了安全滤波器用于引导收缩系统。

Result: 所提出的扰动条件可保持增量指数稳定性，特定条件下可实现渐近收敛；安全滤波器方法在引导系统至期望平衡点并避开不安全集方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文为收缩动力学系统提供了更具一般性的时变扰动分析框架，并展示了其在安全关键控制中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Perturbation theory for asymptotically stable systems has received sustained attention, leading to many criteria that preserve stability under uncertainty. A limitation of classical stability analysis, however, is that it is inherently equilibrium-dependent. Contractive systems, by contrast, are defined independently of any equilibrium and admit a rich set of robustness bounds. In this letter, we develop new time-varying perturbation conditions for contractive dynamics that preserve incremental exponential stability of the perturbed system and, in specific regimes, guarantee asymptotic convergence to solutions of the nominal dynamics. As an application, we propose a safety filter for steering a nominally contractive system to a desired equilibrium while avoiding an unsafe set, and we present an example illustrating advantages of the proposed method over existing approaches.

</details>


### [199] [Particle Methods with Deep Learning for Stochastic Control under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09055)
*Mathieu Laurière,Xiaolu Tan,Jiefei Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于粒子的方法解决部分观测随机控制问题的数值计算，通过有限维加权粒子系统替代无穷维滤波状态，并利用对称神经网络架构发展出两种深度学习算法（直接优化法和Deep BSDE法），在多个数值实验中验证了精度和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分观测下的随机控制问题数值计算困难，因为动态规划在隐藏状态的条件分布上自然表述，而该分布是无穷维的，传统方法难以处理。

Method: 提出基于粒子的方法，用有限维加权粒子系统替代无穷维滤波状态；基于公共噪声适应的平均场控制极限理论证明收敛性；利用粒子系统的置换不变性结构，采用对称神经网络架构；发展两种深度学习算法：直接优化反馈控制法和适用于粒子问题的Deep BSDE法；扩展到部分观测平均场控制问题。

Result: 在适当假设下证明了完全离散化粒子逼近收敛到原始连续时间部分观测控制问题；在线性二次型基准、非线性部分观测平均场控制问题、投资组合清算和资产配置两个金融应用中验证了方法的准确性和实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于粒子的深度学习方法有效解决了部分观测随机控制问题的数值计算挑战，并成功扩展到平均场控制场景，具有良好的理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Numerical computation of stochastic control problems under partial observation is challenging because the dynamic programming formulation is naturally posed on the conditional distribution of the hidden state. We propose particle-based methods that replace this infinite-dimensional filtering state by a finite-dimensional weighted particle system, building on recent limit theory for mean-field control with common-noise-adapted controls. We prove, under suitable assumptions, convergence of the fully discretized particle approximation to the original continuous-time partially observed control problem. The particle reformulation is high-dimensional but permutation-invariant, a structure that can be exploited by symmetric neural network architectures. We develop two deep learning algorithms: a direct optimization method for feedback controls and a Deep BSDE method for particle problems admitting a backward stochastic differential equation representation. We also extend the computational framework to partially observed mean-field control problems, which have been studied theoretically but remain less developed numerically. Numerical experiments on a linear--quadratic benchmark, a nonlinear partially observed mean-field control problem, and two financial applications, portfolio liquidation and asset allocation, demonstrate the accuracy and practical utility of the approach.

</details>


### [200] [Quantitative rapid stabilization for parabolic equations via the linear quadratic theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09063)
*Shengquan Xiang,Yu Xiao,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于线性二次型（LQ）理论，通过选取特殊代价泛函并联合定量可观性不等式，实现了非自伴抛物型方程的定量快速镇定，揭示了线性系统中定量快速镇定与定量可观性不等式之间的等价性，并将该框架应用于Navier-Stokes方程绕非平凡稳态的定量快速镇定，最后扩展到自伴情形下的有限维反馈律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非自伴抛物型方程的定量快速镇定问题，利用LQ理论建立系统化的镇定方法。

Method: 基于LQ理论，选取特殊代价泛函，结合定量可观性不等式，推导定量快速镇定；揭示等价性关系；应用于Navier-Stokes方程；扩展到自伴情形的有限维反馈律。

Result: 实现了非自伴抛物型方程的定量快速镇定；建立了定量快速镇定与定量可观性不等式的等价性；成功应用于Navier-Stokes方程绕非平凡稳态的镇定；在自伴情形下扩展到有限维反馈律。

Conclusion: LQ理论与定量可观性不等式的结合是量化快速镇定的有效工具，且该框架具有通用性，可推广到Navier-Stokes方程等复杂系统及有限维反馈设计。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of quantitative rapid stabilization via the linear quadratic (LQ) theory. Specifically, for non-self-adjoint parabolic equations, by virtue of the LQ theory, we derive the quantitative rapid stabilization by selecting a special cost functional and combining it with a quantitative observability inequality. Furthermore, this approach reveals the equivalence between the quantitative rapid stabilization and the quantitative observability inequality for linear systems. In addition, we apply this framework to the Navier--Stokes equations and establish the quantitative rapid stabilization around nontrivial steady states. Finally, we extend this methodology to finite-dimensional feedback laws in self-adjoint cases.

</details>


### [201] [Beyond Fixed Local Updates: An Adaptive Decentralized Quasi-Newton Method Free of Stepsize Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09070)
*Hao Wu,Liping Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应去中心化拟牛顿法（AdaDQN），通过MM视角重新审视局部更新方法并建立鲁棒非精确算法框架，引入带保护的共识感知终止准则、可扩展的无记忆BFGS更新和事件触发通信协议，克服了现有去中心化算法中步长与局部更新次数成反比的悲观理论瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化算法中，多局部更新的收敛步长必须与局部更新次数成反比，这一理论瓶颈严重限制了算法的计算效率。

Method: 1) 通过Majorization-Minimization视角重新审视局部更新方法，建立鲁棒非精确算法(RIA)框架；2) 提出带保护的共识感知终止准则，动态平衡局部计算增益与共识误差；3) 采用可扩展的无记忆BFGS更新和事件触发通信协议以减少通信开销。

Result: AdaDQN收敛到一阶稳定点，且步长界与最大局部更新次数的倒数解耦；数值实验表明该方法在计算-通信权衡上优于现有最先进的去中心化方法。

Conclusion: AdaDQN成功打破了去中心化优化中步长与局部更新次数之间的理论限制，实现了更优的计算-通信效率权衡。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel Adaptive Decentralized Quasi-Newton (AdaDQN) method for solving smooth nonconvex optimization problems over undirected networks. While existing decentralized algorithms with multiple local updates suffer from a pessimistic theoretical bottleneck, where the convergence stepsize must be inversely proportional to the number of local updates, our work overcomes this limitation. We revisit local update methods through a Majorization-Minimization (MM) lens and establish a Robust Inexact Algorithm (RIA) framework. Inspired by this framework, AdaDQN integrates a safeguarded consensus-aware termination criterion to dynamically balance local computational gains against consensus error, complemented by a scalable memoryless BFGS update and an event-triggered communication protocol to significantly reduce communication overhead. We establish the global convergence of AdaDQN to a first-order stationary point. Crucially, we prove that the convergence stepsize bound is decoupled from the reciprocal of maximum number of local updates. Numerical experiments demonstrate that AdaDQN achieves a superior computation-communication tradeoff, outperforming state-of-the-art decentralized methods across various performance metrics.

</details>


### [202] [Impact of Scaling and Rounding on Metaheuristic Performance for the Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09196)
*Florian Rascoussier,Romain Billot,Lina Fahed,Christine Solnon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了VRPTW问题中浮点数坐标向整数缩放和舍入的方法，分析了不同缩放因子P和三种舍入方案对HGS和OR-Tools两种求解器性能及解质量的影响，并提供了实用建议以提高效率、鲁棒性和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典VRPTW实例使用浮点数坐标导致可重复性和数值一致性问题，现有缩放舍入做法缺乏系统性理解，需要研究不同缩放舍入策略对求解器性能和解质量的影响。

Method: 通过整数缩放因子P和三种舍入方案进行对比实验，在原始浮点实例上评估HGS和OR-Tools两种求解器的性能，并将舍入后的解映射回原始浮点空间进行质量评估。

Result: 缩放和舍入显著影响求解器性能和最终解质量，不同方案在效率和最优性上存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 基于实验结果给出了VRPTW基准测试中缩放和舍入的实用建议，以提升效率、鲁棒性和可重复性。

Abstract: Classical Euclidean instances for the Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (VRPTW) have floating-point arc costs derived from node coordinates. This raises reproducibility and numerical consistency issues. Hence, a common practice is to scale and round data to integer values, yet the impact of these choices is poorly understood. This paper studies the effect of integer scaling by a factor P and compares three rounding schemes with distinct feasibility and optimality guarantees. We analyze how scaling and rounding influence empirical performance of two well-known VRPTW solvers: Hybrid Genetic Search (HGS) and OR-Tools. We evaluate the quality of solutions mapped back to the original floating-point instances. Our results show that scaling and rounding can significantly affect both solver performance and solution quality. Based on these findings, we provide practical recommendations for scaling and rounding in VRPTW benchmarks to improve efficiency, robustness and reproducibility.

</details>


### [203] [Multilevel Stochastic Gradient Descent for Risk-Averse PDE-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09291)
*Niklas Baumgarten,Philipp A. Guth,David Schneiderhan,Tommaso Vanzan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了将多层随机梯度下降算法应用于风险厌恶型三维PDE约束优化问题的最新进展。该算法采用自适应多层蒙特卡洛梯度估计，相比标准批处理随机梯度下降方法，具有更好的并行可扩展性、收敛速度和计算复杂度，并通过三维椭圆扩散问题和大风险厌恶参数验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决风险厌恶型三维PDE约束优化问题中计算复杂度高、收敛速度慢的挑战，弥补标准批处理随机梯度下降方法在并行可扩展性和计算效率方面的不足。

Method: 采用自适应多层蒙特卡洛梯度估计的多层随机梯度下降算法，通过多层次分解和自适应策略提升梯度估计效率，并利用并行计算实现可扩展性。

Result: 在三维椭圆扩散问题和大风险厌恶参数设置下，该方法在收敛速度、计算复杂度和并行可扩展性方面均优于标准批处理随机梯度下降方法。

Conclusion: 多层随机梯度下降算法能有效处理风险厌恶型三维PDE约束优化问题，提供了更优的计算性能和可扩展性，适用于高计算需求场景。

Abstract: We present recent advances in applying and analyzing multilevel stochastic gradient descent algorithms to risk-averse, three-dimensional PDE-constrained optimization problems. The algorithm uses adaptive multilevel Monte Carlo gradient estimates, provides parallel scalability as well as improved convergence rates and computational complexity compared to standard batched stochastic gradient descent methods. We study the method in computationally demanding settings using three-dimensional elliptic diffusion problems and large risk-aversion parameters.

</details>


### [204] [Inverse Problems for Costs and Controls in LQG MFGs via Mean Field Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09302)
*Grégoire Lambrecht,Mathieu Laurière*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于均值-协方差轨迹观测的LQG平均场博弈逆问题，证明了控制的可辨识性条件，并提出了通过半定规划方法恢复一致成本参数的构造性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LQG平均场博弈中，仅通过观测群体均值-协方差轨迹来逆向推断最优控制策略和成本参数，解决参数非注入性带来的辨识挑战。

Method: 首先建立前向纳什均衡存在唯一性；分析参数到轨迹映射的非注入性并给出参数等价的充分条件；提出构造性半定规划方法推断严格一致的代价参数。

Result: 最优控制在观测初始条件下局部可辨识；全局可辨识需要更深层次的结构恢复；半定规划方法能有效推断一致的成本参数。

Conclusion: 建立了LQG-MFG逆问题的理论框架，揭示了参数辨识的局限条件，并提供了可行的参数恢复方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates inverse problems for Linear-Quadratic-Gaussian (LQG) Mean Field Games (MFGs) based entirely on the observation of mean-covariance trajectories. We address three sequential challenges: identifying the optimal control for observed initializations, determining the control for arbitrary initializations, and recovering consistent cost parameters. After establishing the existence and uniqueness of the forward Nash equilibrium under mild hypotheses, we analyze the injectivity of the parameter-to-trajectory mapping, demonstrating that it is inherently non-injective and providing sufficient conditions for parameter equivalence. We prove that while the optimal control is locally identifiable for observed initializations under minimal assumptions, global identifiability requires a deeper structural recovery of the game's costs. To bridge this gap, we propose a constructive semidefinite programming method to infer cost parameters that are strictly consistent with the observed population dynamics. Numerical experiments illustrate this method.

</details>


### [205] [Generalized Cancellation of Capacitor Parasitic Inductance Using a Lattice Network and Its Application to Common-Mode Noise Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09326)
*Katsuya Nomura,Shuhei Chizuwa,Takashi Masuzawa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用格型网络消除电容器寄生电感的通用技术，通过Z矩阵等效变换表明传统垂直对称电感条件仅为充分条件并可放松，进而提出垂直非对称格型网络，仅需调整一个电感即可消除寄生电感，并将该方法扩展至共模噪声抑制，实验验证了两种概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电容器寄生电感会严重影响高频电路性能，传统消除方法要求严格的垂直对称电感条件，实现难度大且灵活性不足，因此需要一种更通用、更简便的寄生电感消除技术。

Method: 基于Z矩阵等效变换分析格型网络，证明传统垂直对称电感条件仅为充分条件；提出垂直非对称格型网络结构，仅需调节单一电感值即可抵消电容寄生电感；并将该方法推广至共模扼流圈用于共模噪声抑制。

Result: 理论与实验均验证了所提出的垂直非对称格型网络能够有效消除电容器寄生电感，且扩展至共模噪声抑制的方案同样有效。

Conclusion: 该技术放宽了传统方法对电感对称性的严格要求，提供了一种更灵活、更简化的寄生电感消除方案，并具备在共模噪声抑制中的应用潜力。

Abstract: This letter presents a generalized technique for cancelling the parasitic inductance of capacitors using a lattice network. The Z-matrix-based equivalent transformation shows that the conventional vertically symmetric inductance condition is only a sufficient condition and can be relaxed. A vertically asymmetric lattice network is then proposed, enabling the parasitic inductance of capacitors to be cancelled by adjusting only one inductance. The method is further extended to common-mode noise reduction using a common-mode choke, and both concepts are experimentally verified.

</details>


### [206] [Second order Taylor-like topological expansion with multiple holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09363)
*Kevin Sturm*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单孔拓扑展开推导多孔形状泛函拓扑展开的框架。在二维情况下，该展开可类比球体积的泰勒展开；三维情况则呈现不同的几何特性，即使无PDE约束也会出现维度依赖行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种系统性的框架，将形状泛函关于多孔（多个空洞）的拓扑展开从单孔情形推广而来，类似于通过多次一维泰勒展开得到多维泰勒展开的思路。

Method: 在二维中，将多孔拓扑展开与球体积的泰勒展开相关联，用拓扑状态导数替代通常的导数。引入第二拓扑状态导数（异点处定义为拓扑状态导数的拓扑状态导数，同点处定义为复合层展开的"远场"分量）。三维中仍使用第一、第二拓扑状态导数，但展开模式不同于二维的泰勒展开类比。

Result: 二维情况下，多孔拓扑展开可类比为关于球体积的泰勒展开；三维情况下，拓扑展开虽然仍涉及一阶和二阶拓扑状态导数，但不再遵循球体积的泰勒展开模式，这种维度依赖源于几何本质。

Conclusion: 拓扑展开的维度依赖性是一种几何特性，即便在没有PDE约束的代价泛函中也会体现，说明二维和三维的多孔拓扑展开结构存在根本性差异。

Abstract: In this paper we derive a framework for topological expansions of shape functionals with respect to multiple holes based on the expansion of one hole. Our strategy is similar to deriving a Taylor expansion of order two of functions in $\mathbf R^d$ by applying $d$-times a Taylor expansion in $\mathbf R$.
  For shape functionals defined on subsets of $\mathbf R^2$ we relate the topological expansion with multiple holes to a Taylor expansion with respect to the ball volumes where usual derivatives are replaced by topological state derivatives. The first topological state derivative was introduced in a previous paper and relates the first topological derivative of cost functionals. The second topological state derivative at two distinct points is introduced, similar to a second directional derivatives of functions defined on Banach spaces, as the topological state derivative of the topological state derivative. The second topological state derivative at the same point is defined as the "far-away" component of the compound layer expansion of the state variable.
  In dimension three the obtained topological expansion still involves first and second topological state derivatives, but it does not follow the same pattern of a Taylor expansion in the ball volumes. As we will show this dimension dependent behaviour is rather of geometrical nature and already appears in cost functionals without PDE constraints.

</details>


### [207] [Benchmarking Deep Time Series Models for Equity Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09420)
*Aoxin Zhang,Yuhan Cheng,Kwanting Leung*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了2018-2024年CRSP日频股票基准，比较15种深度学习和统计时间序列架构的日频权益组合预测性能。评估采用组合窗口十分位组合、随机多准则可接受性分析（SMAA）、部署调整可接受性指数及含容量、贝塔、行业、风险、杠杆、换手率约束的二次规划组合层。结果显示，TransEnc-8在rank-1可接受性上最高（0.352），但无模型超过0.36；排名因偏好、市场状态、特征空间和交易成本而异；在约束组合下，所有模型的净夏普比率（20bps成本）均为负值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试仅关注预测准确性，忽略了现实投资中偏好、成本和组合约束对模型实际可用性的影响。需要建立一个同时评估预测性能与部署后实际效用的综合基准框架。

Method: 采用15种深度和统计时间序列架构，在CRSP日频股票数据上构建2018-2024年基准。评估协议包含：共同窗口十分位组合、随机多准则可接受性分析（SMAA）、部署调整可接受性指数（从SMAA秩可接受性分布出发，对准则级胜出但产生高组合后悔的模型给予降权，其Gibbs形式为SMAA先验的熵更新），以及含容量、贝塔、行业、风险、杠杆、换手率约束的二次规划组合层。

Result: 无单一架构在原始基准上占主导地位。TransEnc-8的rank-1可接受性最高（0.352），所有模型不超过0.36。排名随偏好、市场状态、特征空间和交易成本变化。在五模型约束组合比较中，TransEnc-8始终被选中，而收益导向的原始排名可能偏好TS-RIDGE。宽特征空间的十分位信号能承受交易成本，但所有推广模型在20bps成本下的约束QP净夏普比率均为负值。

Conclusion: 该基准支持模型选择与诊断，而非独立的交易策略主张。仅考虑预测准确性的原始排名不足以评估模型的实际部署价值，必须纳入偏好、约束和交易成本等现实因素。

Abstract: Benchmarking forecasting architectures for daily equity portfolios is not just a prediction exercise. It also asks which model remains usable after preferences, costs, and portfolio constraints are imposed. We build a CRSP daily-stock benchmark for 15 deep and statistical time-series architectures over 2018--2024. The protocol combines common-window decile portfolios, stochastic multi-criteria acceptability analysis, a deployment-adjusted acceptability index, and a constrained quadratic portfolio layer with capacity, beta, industry, risk, leverage, and turnover controls. The index starts from the SMAA rank-acceptability distribution and downweights models whose criteria-level wins produce high portfolio regret; its Gibbs form is characterized as an entropic update from the SMAA prior. Empirically, no architecture dominates the raw benchmark: TransEnc-8 has the largest rank-1 acceptability, 0.352, and no model exceeds about 0.36. Rankings vary with preferences, market state, feature universe, and transaction costs. In the promoted five-model constrained-portfolio comparison, TransEnc-8 is selected throughout, while return-oriented raw rankings can favor TS-RIDGE. Broad-universe decile signals can survive costs, but the baseline constrained-QP net Sharpe at 20 bps is negative for every promoted model. The benchmark supports model selection and diagnosis rather than a standalone trading-strategy claim.

</details>


### [208] [Metric-Free Riemannian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09465)
*Jonas Püschel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了黎曼优化中无需显式应用度量的方法，证明许多一阶优化组件仅依赖于目标函数的微分和黎曼梯度，而不需要显式度量计算，并基于此发展了无度量公式，将优化推广到Finsler和Banach流形。实验表明该方法在保持有效性的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多应用中，显式计算或应用黎曼度量在计算上昂贵或数值不稳定，限制了黎曼优化算法的实际效率，因此需要探索能否在不显式应用度量的情况下进行黎曼优化。

Method: 论文首先分析黎曼优化中一阶组件的依赖关系，证明它们仅依赖目标函数的微分和黎曼梯度而非显式度量应用；然后基于此发展无度量公式，并将优化方法推广到Finsler和Banach流形。

Result: 提出的无度量策略在保持与依赖度量的方法相同有效性的同时，显著降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: 黎曼优化的很大一部分可以独立于显式度量应用进行，这拓宽了其在度量昂贵或隐式定义问题中的适用性。

Abstract: Riemannian optimization provides a powerful framework for constrained optimization by incorporating problem-specific structure directly into the geometry of the search space. In many applications, however, the explicit evaluation or application of the Riemannian metric can be computationally expensive or numerically unstable, limiting the practical efficiency of otherwise well-founded algorithms. Motivated by such settings, this work investigates to what extent classical Riemannian optimization algorithms can be reformulated without explicitly applying the metric. We show that many first-order components of Riemannian optimization only rely on the differential of the objective function and access to the Riemannian gradient, but not on explicit metric application. Based on this observation, we develop metric-free formulations and generalize optimization approaches to Finsler and Banach manifolds. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed metric-free strategies retain the effectiveness of their metric-dependent counterparts while significantly reducing computational overhead. These results highlight that a substantial portion of Riemannian optimization can be carried out independently of explicit metric application, broadening its applicability to problems with expensive or implicitly defined metrics.

</details>


### [209] [Complete Trajectory Tracking for Polynomial Differential Variational Inequalities: A Moment-SOS Based Structure-Aware Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09609)
*Zhang Huang,Wei Li,Jie Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多项式微分变分不等式（PDVIs），提出了一种结构感知的SOS-PDVIRK4算法，该算法结合了四阶Runge-Kutta积分器与Moment-SOS层次结构，利用参数空间的半代数分层来处理解的多重性和结构转换，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式微分变分不等式是非光滑动力学系统的重要类别，现有方法在处理参数空间中解的结构性变化和多解性方面存在不足，需要建立系统的理论框架和高效的数值算法。

Method: 首先对参数多项式变分不等式进行分层分析，证明参数空间可划分为有限个不相交的半代数区域；在此基础上提出结构感知的SOS-PDVIRK4算法，将四阶Runge-Kutta积分器与Moment-SOS层次结构相结合，利用参数空间几何结构指导混合策略来处理解的多重性。

Result: 建立了参数空间半代数分层的理论结果，证明了在几乎处处初始条件下PDVI最大正则解的存在性；数值实验表明从相同初始状态但不同初始控制出发的轨迹存在显著分岔，揭示了参数多项式VI解映射的不连续结构。

Conclusion: 提出的结构感知框架能够有效处理PDVI系统中的解多重性和结构转换问题，为分析约束条件下复杂动力学系统的长期行为提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper investigates Polynomial Differential Variational Inequalities (PDVIs), a class of non-smooth dynamical systems in which an ordinary differential equation is coupled with a time-dependent variational inequality (VI) defined by polynomials. As a foundation for polynomial differential variational inequalities, we investigate the parametric polynomial variational inequality and establish that, under mild assumptions, its parameter space admits a stratification into finitely many disjoint semi-algebraic regions, each corresponding to a qualitatively distinct solution structure. Furthermore, we establish the existence of maximal regular solutions to the PDVI for almost every initial condition in the parametric feasible set, where each solution has finitely many switches and evolves according to continuous semi-algebraic selection laws between switching times. Leveraging the structured partition of the parameter space, in which region boundaries correspond to discontinuities in the solution map of the parametric VI, we propose the structure-aware SOS-PDVIRK4 algorithm to handle solution multiplicity and structural transitions. This method integrates a fourth-order Runge--Kutta integrator with the Moment--SOS hierarchy and features a novel hybrid strategy that is explicitly guided by the underlying parameter space geometry. The efficacy of our structure-aware framework is demonstrated through numerical experiments on a constrained cyber-physical control system. Our numerical experiments demonstrate that, from identical initial states but different initial controls, trajectories can diverge significantly, revealing the discontinuous structure of the solution map of the parametric polynomial VI. The experiments also include numerical convergence studies and highlight the potential of our algorithm to explore the long-term behavior of PDVI systems.

</details>


### [210] [Powering the Future of AI: Navigating the Trade-offs for Europe's Energy Transition and Net-Zero Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09617)
*Mohammad Hemmati,Gbemi Oluleye,Vassilis M. Charitopoulos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用空间显式优化模型，在21种AI增长情景下系统量化了欧洲超大规模数据中心对电力系统的额外需求、容量、排放和运行影响。研究发现AI到2050年可能驱动73-723 TWh额外电力需求，导致2030-2050年间累计排放超调67-181 MtCO2。2030年后AI基础设施布局将更多受制于可靠电力和系统灵活性而非清洁能源丰裕度，并在中间年份存在关键排放风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI的全球快速扩张导致高能耗的超大规模数据中心激增，使其成为电力系统规划与运行中结构性的挑战，亟需系统量化其对电力需求、容量、排放和运行的影响。

Method: 采用空间显式优化模型，构建21种AI增长情景，对欧洲电力系统进行建模，量化分析数据中心带来的额外电力需求、装机容量、碳排放及运行影响。

Result: (1) AI到2050年可驱动73-723 TWh额外需求；(2) 2030-2050年累计排放超调67-181 MtCO2；(3) 中等情景下AI需额外200小时可靠发电，LCOE增加35 EUR/MWh；(4) 悲观情景下需70 GW新增容量，优化增长路径下可达226 GW；(5) 工作负载动态显著影响调度、灵活性和排放，效率提升可降低容量需求和系统峰值。

Conclusion: 虽然2050年净零目标可能实现，但中间年份存在关键排放风险，欧盟若不适时调整政策以适应加速的数字转型，可能危及碳中和目标。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of AI globally has led to the proliferation of energy-intensive hyperscale data centres (DCs), making them as a structurally challenging component in power system planning and operation. Using a spatially explicit optimisation model of Europe across 21 AI growth scenarios, we systematically quantify additional demand, capacity requirements, emissions, and operational impacts of DCs. Results indicate that AI could drive 73-723 TWh of extra demand by 2050, risking cumulative emissions overshoots of 67-181 MtCO2 between 2030 and 2050. Our analysis indicates that after 2030, the geography of AI infrastructure will be shaped more by firm power and system flexibility than by the mere abundance of clean energy. In moderate scenarios, AI requires an additional of 200 hours of firm generation, which increases LCOE by 35 EUR/MWh in key hubs. We show that even under the pessimistic scenarios, existing infrastructure would require 70 GW additional capacity, while under managed growth pathways, this expansion could reach 226 GW. We further find DCs workload dynamics strongly shape energy dispatch, system flexibility, and emissions, while improved efficiency significantly reduces capacity needs, and system peaks. While our findings suggest that net-zero targets for 2050 may be achieved, critical emission risks may appear in the intermediate years, and the EU may compromise its carbon-neutral goals unless policies adapt to this accelerating digital transformation.

</details>


### [211] [Mean field games with option to buy information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09784)
*Bernardo D'Auria,Markus Fischer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类连续时间有限时域平均场博弈模型，其中代表性玩家的目标函数依赖于隐藏状态、位置、控制和总体分布。玩家可以选择付费观察隐藏状态，同时作用于位置动态。研究将模型与含自由停止的最优控制平均场模型联系起来，刻画解的特征，给出显式可解示例，并证明在兼容信息结构下可从极限模型解构造N人博弈的近似纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均场博弈模型通常假设玩家的目标函数完全基于可观测状态，未考虑隐藏状态对决策的影响。本文旨在引入隐藏状态依赖，并赋予玩家付费获取隐藏信息的可选能力，从而更真实地建模信息不对称下的群体决策问题。

Method: 采用连续时间有限时域平均场博弈框架，将原问题与含自由停止的最优控制平均场模型建立联系；通过特征刻画和显式可解示例分析模型性质；并基于兼容信息结构，从极限模型解构造N人博弈的近似纳什均衡。

Result: 成功连接了隐藏状态依赖的平均场博弈与含自由停止的最优控制模型；给出了模型的显式可解示例；证明了在兼容信息结构下，可从极限模型解构造N人博弈的近似纳什均衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为含隐藏状态和信息获取选项的平均场博弈提供了理论框架和解的刻画方法，并建立了从连续极限到有限玩家博弈的近似均衡构造路径。

Abstract: We introduce a class of continuous time finite horizon mean field games where the objective function of the representative player depends on a hidden state, in addition to position, control, and the population distribution. While acting on the position dynamics, the agent has the option to pay for seeing the hidden state. We connect the original formulation of our model with a mean field model of optimal control with discretionary stopping, characterize solutions, and give a simple explicitly solvable example. For a class of $N$-player games with compatible information structure, we show that approximate Nash equilibria can be constructed starting from a solution to the limit model.

</details>


### [212] [Limit Theory for $N$-Player $α$-Potential Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09815)
*Xin Guo,Meng Wang,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了α-势博弈当玩家数量趋于无穷大时的极限行为，证明了势平均场博弈（MFGs）自然产生于此极限过程，并建立了有限玩家博弈与平均场控制问题之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-势博弈框架为分析有限玩家动态博弈提供了工具，但其在大规模极限下的行为尚未被充分理解。本文旨在建立α-势博弈与平均场博弈之间的理论联系，为构造势MFGs提供统一方法。

Method: 通过分析归一化N玩家α_N-势函数的最优值和最小化器在N→∞时的收敛性；利用Wasserstein空间中的微分几何技术构造MFGs的势函数；建立lim_{N→∞}α_N=0与现有势MFG条件的等价性。

Result: 证明了有限玩家α-势博弈在N→∞时收敛到具有测度值控制的平均场控制（MFC）问题；lim_{N→∞}α_N=0等价于势MFG的存在条件；极限MFC问题的目标函数可作为对应MFGs的势函数；建立了带共同噪声和非可分离控制交互的扩散过程的混沌传播性质。

Conclusion: 本文建立了α-势博弈与平均场博弈之间的桥梁，为从有限玩家博弈通过渐近条件构造势MFGs提供了新方法，并推广了混沌传播理论到更一般的控制扩散过程。

Abstract: The framework of $α$-potential games has recently been introduced as a tool to analyze finite-player dynamic games, reducing the challenging task of finding approximate Nash equilibria to a control problem of minimizing a single function called $α$-potential function. In this work, we investigate the limiting behavior of $α$-potential games as the number of players $N$ tends to infinity. We show that potential mean field games (MFGs) arise naturally as this limit. Specifically, both the optimal values and the minimizers of normalized $N$-player $α_N$-potential functions converge to those of a mean field control (MFC) problem with measure-valued controls. We establish the equivalence of $\lim_{N\to\infty}α_N= 0$ with the existing conditions for potential MFGs, and provide an unified approach to construct the potential function for MFGs using the techniques from differential geometry in Wasserstein space. We further demonstrate that the objective of the limiting MFC problem serves as a potential function for the corresponding MFGs, an extension of the analogous finite-player setting. This connection yields new constructions of potential MFGs from a finite-player game, through the asymptotic condition $\lim_{N\to \infty}α_N= 0$. As a by-product, we establish propagation of chaos for $N$-player games converging to MFGs for general controlled diffusions with common noise and non-separable control interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Plasma Control in Nuclear Fusion: Codebase and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07550)
*Yang Fu,Haomin Bao,Rohit Sonker,Xiaoyan Hu,Aravind Venugopal,Jeff Schneider,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RL4F，一个用于核聚变等离子体控制的离线强化学习基准，包含基于DIII-D托卡马克历史数据的闭环评估环境和四个全轮廓追踪任务，实验表明离线模型基RL方法平均表现最佳，但无单一方法在所有任务中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习为从历史托卡马克数据开发等离子体控制器提供了可行途径，但由于缺乏标准化基准，难以衡量该方向的进展。

Method: 构建了基于DIII-D托卡马克历史放电数据的动力学评估环境，设计了旋转、密度、温度和压力四个全轮廓追踪任务，在统一协议下评估了多种模仿学习和离线强化学习基线方法。

Result: 离线模型基RL方法在多数目标上获得最佳平均性能，但无单一方法在所有任务中占优。

Conclusion: RL4F为核聚变社区和离线RL算法开发提供了标准化基准，强调了动力学建模在复杂长时域等离子体控制任务中的重要性。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising route for developing plasma controllers from historical tokamak data, since online trial-and-error on real devices is costly and risky. However, progress in this direction remains difficult to measure due to the lack of a standardized offline RL benchmark for realistic multi-actuator, long-horizon plasma control problems in nuclear fusion. We introduce RL4F, an Offline Reinforcement Learning Benchmark for Plasma Control in Nuclear Fusion, providing closed-loop evaluation environments and baseline comparisons across four full-profile tracking tasks: rotation, density, temperature, and pressure. The dynamics function underlying the evaluation environment is built from historical discharge data from DIII-D, a real-world Tokamak. We evaluate a broad set of imitation learning and offline RL baselines under a unified protocol. We find that offline model-based RL methods obtain the best average performance on most objectives, although no single method dominates all tasks, highlighting the importance of dynamics modeling in complex, long-horizon plasma control tasks. To foster further research, we open-source the codebase, datasets, and evaluation framework, providing a benchmark not only for the fusion community but also for algorithm development in offline RL.

</details>


### [214] [MedicalRec: Medical recommender system for image classification without retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07553)
*Roghayeh Taghavi,Aysa Hasanazde Bashkandi,Amir Ali Bengari,Mohammad Amin Raji,Mohammad Salahi Ardekani,Parisa Mardukhian,Parvaneh Rezaei,Ramin Mousa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Transformer的推荐系统MedicalRec，用于为医学图像分类任务推荐最优模型。系统基于从3000篇论文中收集的包含5000多条记录的数据集MedicalRec-Bench进行训练，在四种特征配置下评估，最高HitRate@100达到75.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习和深度学习在医疗领域广泛应用，但模型选择通常依赖试错法，导致大量能源消耗和碳排放。研究者希望开发一个推荐系统，帮助研究人员直接选择最适合其医学图像分类任务的模型，避免不必要的计算资源浪费。

Method: 研究者从3000篇医学图像分类论文中收集数据，构建了包含5000多条记录的MedicalRec-Bench数据集，涵盖皮肤癌、肿瘤、伤口、乳腺癌和MRI等分类任务。数据集按特征数量分为四个模式（5/9/11/18个特征）。由于作者未报告完整信息，数据存在大量缺失值。模型采用基于Transformer的架构进行项目推荐。

Result: MedicalRec在数据集评估和与12个基模型的对比中表现优异，最大HitRate@100达到75.5%。数据集和实现代码已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: MedicalRec作为一种模型推荐系统，能够有效帮助研究人员为医学图像分类任务选择最优模型，减少试错带来的能源消耗和计算资源浪费。

Abstract: The emergence of machine learning and deep learning has revolutionized the efficiency of diagnostic, therapeutic, and administrative systems in healthcare. However, this rapid adoption has come at the cost of requiring significant computing power and energy consumption, as well as e-waste disposal and carbon emissions. One of the challenges of these models is choosing the right model for classification tasks. To this end, researchers attempt to identify the optimal model using their data through trial and error, which involves energy consumption and waste. The goal of this study is to develop a model-based recommender system for medical image classification. For this purpose, a data set was collected from 3,000 articles in the field of medical image classification. This dataset, publicly available under the name MedicalRec-Bench, contains over 5,000 records of models tested in various tasks, including Skin Cancer Classification, Tumour Classification, Wound Classification, Breast Cancer, and MRI classification. The dataset was evaluated in four different modes, depending on the number of features: MedicalRec I (5 features), MedicalRec II (9 features), MedicalRec III (11 features), and MedicalRec IV (18 features). Collecting all values for the features is challenging due to non-reporting by the authors; hence, the dataset contains significant amounts of missing values. The Medical Recommender System (MedicalRec) is a transformer-based model used for item recommendations in this study. This model achieved remarkable results in the evaluation on the dataset and in the evaluation with 12 base models. This model achieved a maximum HitRate@100 of 75.5%. The dataset and implementations are available through the GitHub link: https://github.com/Ramin1Mousa/MedicalRec

</details>


### [215] [SPIN: Decentralized Swarm Control via Tensorized Policy Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07557)
*Zhaowen Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPIN框架，通过张量网络分解（MPS链）将多智能体联合策略复杂度从指数级降至线性级，并采用离线预训练+运行时零-shot重加权（Radon-Nikodým导数）的混合神经符号控制管线，实现资源受限边缘平台上的可扩展群体智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化多智能体群体协同在资源受限的边缘平台上，面临联合动作空间指数级扩展和高延迟通信开销的根本性瓶颈。

Method: 1) 将群体拓扑建模为压缩张量网络，利用矩阵乘积态（MPS）链分解局部多智能体clique的联合策略张量，复杂度从指数O(n^m)降至线性O(m·n·χ²)。2) 提出解耦的混合神经符号控制管线：离线预训练局部多层神经网络作为结构协调编码器，将手工几何描述符映射为环境目标度量；运行时边缘智能体通过Radon-Nikodým导数进行零-shot重要性重加权滤波，实现瞬时行为自适应。

Result: 在离散时间多智能体仿真沙箱中，覆盖跟踪、去中心化分散/区域覆盖和多目标协调等场景。定性遥测显示：稳定的目标导向运动、去中心化约束下的抗坍塌空间扩散、跨多目标的结构化子群形成。

Conclusion: SPIN框架为低功耗边缘群体智能提供了一条数学上严谨的可行路径，实现了可计算性与实际部署能力的平衡。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-agent swarm coordination on resource-constrained edge platforms remains fundamentally bottlenecked by the exponential scaling of joint action spaces and high-latency communication overhead. This paper introduces the Swarm Policy Interference Network (SPIN) framework, an architectural paradigm that bypasses these limitations by modeling swarm topologies as a compressed tensor network. We factorize the joint policy tensors of local multi-agent cliques into Matrix Product State (MPS) chains, reducing the computational complexity of evaluation from an exponential $O(n^m)$ wall to a strictly linear $O(m \cdot n \cdot χ^2)$ constraint. To bridge local continuous spatial geometry with this discrete algebraic backend without requiring power-intensive online training loops, we introduce a decoupled, hybrid neuro-symbolic control pipeline. Local multi-layered neural networks operate as structural coordination encoders, pre-trained offline to nonlinearly map hand-engineered geometric descriptors into abstract environmental target measures. At runtime, edge agents execute instantaneous behavioral adaptations by applying the Radon-Nikodým derivative directly as a zero-shot importance-reweighting filter. We validate the framework within a discrete-time multi-agent simulation sandbox spanning tracking, decentralized dispersion/area coverage, and multi-goal coordination regimes. Qualitative telemetry demonstrates that the integrated pipeline achieves stable target-directed motion, anti-collapse spatial spreading under decentralized constraints, and structured subgroup formation across multiple targets, providing a mathematically grounded route to tractable, low-power edge swarm intelligence.

</details>


### [216] [Boundary Variance Inflation Causes Acquisition Bias in Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07561)
*Maria Bånkestad,Sanna Jarl,Jens Sjölund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了有界域上高斯过程平稳核在边界处后验方差膨胀的根本原因——核相关邻域在边界处的截断，并展示了这种几何畸变如何导致三种采集函数产生不同的边界偏好模式，最后提出了无函数依赖的选择轮廓诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管边界处后验方差膨胀是地质统计学中长期被认识到的伪影，也是贝叶斯优化中过度探索的来源，但其根本原因和边界诱导的采集偏差的影响机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过理论分析将边界偏差追溯到核相关邻域在域边界截断这一简单几何机制，并分别在方差最大化、负积分后验方差和期望预测信息增益三类采集函数下分析畸变表现模式，提出无函数依赖的选择轮廓诊断方法。

Result: 发现方差最大化将选择集中在角落，而负积分后验方差和期望预测信息增益将选择向内移至轴向内部壳层；这些模式与目标函数无关，采集行为可能被核几何主导而非任务相关的不确定性。

Conclusion: 边界核截断导致的几何畸变随维度增加而恶化，且采集偏差可独立于目标函数出现，所提出的诊断方法适用于任意采集函数、核函数和有界域几何。

Abstract: Gaussian processes with stationary kernels on bounded domains exhibit inflated posterior variance near the boundary. Despite being a long-recognized artifact in geostatistics and a source of over-exploration in Bayesian optimization, the causes and effects of boundary-induced acquisition bias are underexplored. We trace the root cause to a simple geometric mechanism: the truncation of the kernel correlation neighborhood at the domain boundary creates an observation-independent distortion that worsens with dimensionality. We show how this distortion manifests across three acquisition classes: variance maximization concentrates selections at the corners, whereas negative integrated posterior variance and expected predictive information gain move selections inward to axis-aligned interior shells. These patterns arise without reference to any objective function, meaning that acquisition behavior can be dominated by kernel geometry rather than the desired task-specific uncertainty. To quantify this, we introduce a function-free selection-profile diagnostic for arbitrary acquisitions, kernels, and bounded-domain geometries.

</details>


### [217] [Emergence via Phase Transitions: Mechanism Landscapes and Universal Convergence Across Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07563)
*Truong Xuan Khanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层级涌现框架（HEF），将涌现建模为机制景观中的相变，受热力学和信息论约束。在111项模算术变换器实验中发现，权重范数在突现泛化（grokking）前系统性达到峰值（92%运行），归一化精度曲线坍缩到tanh扭结（R²=0.93），所有突现模型收敛到相同精度水平（0.9745±0.014），与初始化等条件无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习和生物学等领域中，独立演化系统常收敛到相似高层结构（如突现电路、代谢方案、重正化流固定点）。作者希望提出一个可证伪的统一框架来解释这种趋同现象。

Method: 提出层级涌现框架（HEF），引入临界能量阈值Ec，将涌现视为机制景观中的相变，低于Ec为探索竞争态，高于Ec为唯一最小成本机制主导态。通过111项模算术变换器实验验证，监测权重范数、归一化精度等指标。

Result: 92%运行中权重范数在突现前系统性达到峰值；归一化精度曲线坍缩到tanh扭结（R²=0.93），符合Landau-Ginzburg普适类；所有突现模型收敛到0.9745±0.014精度，与初始化等条件无关（ANOVA p>0.13）。

Conclusion: HEF不是通用涌现理论，而是可证伪的数学框架，为研究跨复杂系统的趋同现象提供结构化工具。

Abstract: Across machine learning, biology, and physics, independently evolving systems often converge toward strikingly similar high-level structures despite radically different microscopic details. Grokking circuits converge across random seeds, evolutionary lineages rediscover similar metabolic solutions, and renormalization flows approach common fixed points. We propose the Hierarchical Emergence Framework (HEF) as a candidate universality framework for such convergence phenomena. HEF models emergence as a phase transition in a mechanism landscape constrained by thermodynamic and information-theoretic laws. The framework introduces a critical energy threshold Ec separating an exploration regime with competing mechanisms from a convergence regime governed by a unique minimum-cost mechanism. Under structural assumptions, we prove physical feasibility, derive strict metric contraction, and establish convergence toward a unique fixed-point representation independent of initial conditions. We further connect this convergence structure to causal emergence through Effective Information and mechanism competition entropy. To test the framework, we study delayed generalization ("grokking") in modular arithmetic transformers across 111 experiments. We identify a reproducible empirical fingerprint of the Ec transition: the weight norm peaks systematically before grokking in 92% of runs. Normalized accuracy curves collapse onto a tanh kink (R^2=0.93) consistent with a Landau-Ginzburg universality class, and all grokked models converge to 0.9745+/-0.014 regardless of initialization, weight decay, or training fraction (ANOVA p>0.13). HEF is not presented as a universal theory of emergence, but as a falsifiable mathematical scaffold for studying convergence phenomena across complex systems.

</details>


### [218] [STARIXNet: Multivariate and Multi-attribute Deep Learning Approach to Real-Time Resource Allocation in Cloud Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07565)
*Ahmed Abdulaal,Maruf Aytekin,Thilaga kumaran Srinivasan,Tomer Lancewicki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STARIXNet是一种轻量级神经网络，通过捕捉多维系统指标间的时空关系来指导微服务资源分配决策，优先考虑服务稳定性而非原始预测精度，在Walmart生产环境中实现10%-50%的成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微服务扩缩容方案局限于单变量空间（通常仅依赖CPU使用率），且将问题视为纯预测任务而忽略低估风险和系统响应延迟，替代方案则计算复杂难以大规模实时部署。

Method: STARIXNet建模多个准依赖属性——季节性(S)、时间性(T)、自回归集成(AR)、外生性(X)模式，然后实现聚合策略以确定扩缩容决策，优先考虑服务稳定性，其次才是成本效率和原始预测精度。

Result: 在Walmart关键生产微服务中部署，实现10%-50%的可量化成本节省，以及服务稳定性和客户体验提升等无形收益。

Conclusion: STARIXNet通过多维时空建模和以稳定性优先的决策策略，有效解决了云平台微服务智能扩缩容中预测精度与系统响应延迟之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Intelligent scaling of microservices in cloud platforms is crucial for mitigating escalating compute costs while avoiding service disruptions. Current solutions are limited to the univariate space, typically focusing on CPU usage alone to drive scaling decisions. Moreover, they address the problem as a purely forecasting task, focusing on prediction precision while neglecting the greater risks of underestimation and delays in system responsiveness. Alternative solutions are computationally complex, making them impractical for large-scale, real-time deployments. To address these challenges, we present STARIXNet, a lightweight neural network that guides resource allocation decisions in the multivariate space by capturing spatio-temporal relationships among multiple system metrics. STARIXNet models multiple quasi-dependent attributes, in particular the (S)easonal, (T)emporal, (A)uto-(R)egressive (I)ntegrated, and e(X)ogenous patterns, then implements an aggregation policy to finalize scaling decisions, prioritizing service stability, followed by cost-efficiency, over raw forecast accuracy. We empirically demonstrate the performance of STARIXNet by benchmarking against existing solutions in real-world settings. STARIXNet is deployed for critical production microservices at Walmart achieving tangible savings ranging from 10\% to 50\%, in addition to intangible benefits through improved service stability and customer experience.

</details>


### [219] [TriHead-GAN: A Generative Adversarial Network with Triple-Head Discriminator for Carbon Emission Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07569)
*Zesen Wang,Lijuan Lan,Yonggang Li,Chunhua Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对城市级碳排放高频数据稀缺限制深度学习模型的问题，提出TriHead-GAN框架，通过三重鉴别器分别监督分布真实性、跨变量依赖性和步态时序平滑性，在多个数据集上优于主流基准，并提升了低资源场景下的下游预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级碳排放高频监测数据极度稀缺，严重限制了数据驱动的深度学习模型；现有时序生成方法（GAN、扩散模型）对碳排放数据领域结构的显式监督不足，无法充分保留CO₂与共排放污染物及气象因素间的跨变量相关性，且生成的序列缺乏真实的步态波动特性。

Method: 提出TriHead-GAN，一种基于Transformer的对抗框架，其三重鉴别器联合监督联合分布的三个方面：通过Wasserstein判别器监督分布真实性、通过无泄露回归监督跨变量依赖性、通过相邻差分预测监督步态时序平滑性。生成器结合全局自注意力与局部时序卷积、逐步噪声注入以及匹配一阶差分统计量的抗平滑损失。

Result: 在自采集的长沙碳排放数据集、两个公开碳排放数据集（中国、美国）以及ETTh1基准上，TriHead-GAN在绝大多数设置下优于主流基线，且生成的合成窗口提升了低资源碳排放监测场景中的下游预测准确性。

Conclusion: TriHead-GAN通过三重鉴别器的显式监督有效解决了碳排放时序生成中跨变量依赖性和步态波动保持的挑战，为碳监测数据稀缺问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate carbon emission monitoring is critical for climate policy and emerging regulatory mechanisms such as the EU Carbon Border Adjustment Mechanism, yet city-level high-frequency monitoring data remain extremely scarce, severely limiting data-hungry deep learning models. Time series generation is a natural remedy, but existing GAN and diffusion-based generators often provide limited explicit supervision for the domain structure of carbon emission data: they may match marginal distributional statistics while insufficiently preserving cross-variable correlations between CO$_2$ and co-emitted pollutants and meteorological factors, and tend to collapse the first-difference statistics of atmospheric measurements, producing sequences that are smooth on average but lack the realistic step-wise variability of the underlying signals. We propose TriHead-GAN, a Transformer-based adversarial framework whose triple-head discriminator jointly supervises three complementary aspects of the joint distribution: distributional authenticity via a Wasserstein critic, cross-variable dependency via leakage-free regression of the target variable, and step-wise temporal smoothness via adjacent-difference prediction. The generator combines global self-attention with local temporal convolution, per-step noise injection, and an anti-smoothing loss that matches first-difference statistics. Experiments on the self-collected Changsha Carbon dataset, two public carbon datasets (China, US), and the ETTh1 benchmark show that TriHead-GAN achieves favorable performance over mainstream baselines on the vast majority of settings, and that the resulting synthetic windows improve downstream forecasting accuracy in low-resource carbon monitoring scenarios.

</details>


### [220] [Enabling KV Caching of Shared Prefix for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07571)
*Younghun Go,Jaehoon Han,Changyong Shin,Chuk Yoo,Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双向前缀缓存（bicache）技术，解决扩散语言模型（DLM）中共享前缀KV缓存因双向注意力机制导致的精度崩溃问题，在保证精度的同时提升服务吞吐量36.3%-98.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）采用双向注意力机制，任何token更新都会动态改变整个上下文及其KV值，导致传统LLM的KV缓存技术（假设KV不变）在DLM中精度崩溃至接近零。需要针对DLM设计新的共享前缀KV缓存方法以实现高吞吐服务。

Method: 基于关键观察：共享前缀KV在浅层保持稳定可重用，且浅层深度取决于请求中共享前缀token的比例。bicache动态识别可重用共享前缀KV的安全层深度，消除冗余计算。

Result: 相比现有技术，吞吐量提升36.3%-98.3%，精度仅下降0-1.8%，无精度崩溃问题。

Conclusion: bicache是首个针对DLM共享前缀的KV缓存技术，通过动态识别安全重用深度有效解决双向注意力带来的KV失效问题，实现高吞吐DLM服务。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching for shared prefixes is essential for high-throughput large language model (LLM) serving, but it faces critical challenges in emerging diffusion language models (DLMs). In DLMs, bidirectional attention means that updating any token dynamically alters the entire context and its corresponding KVs. Thus, existing caching techniques developed for LLMs, which assume that KVs remain invariant once computed, corrupt the shared prefix KVs. Our experiments show that applying these techniques to DLMs causes model accuracy to collapse to near zero.
  To unlock high-throughput DLM serving, we propose bidirectional prefix caching, bicache, the first KV caching technique for shared prefixes in DLMs. bicache is designed based on key observations from our comprehensive analysis: shared prefix KVs remain stable and reusable in shallow layers, while the depth of shallow layers depends on the fraction of shared prefix tokens in each request. Thus, bicache dynamically identifies a safe layer depth for reusing shared prefix KVs and eliminates redundant computation. Evaluations demonstrate that bicache significantly improves serving throughput by 36.3%-98.3% compared to existing techniques without accuracy collapse (only 0-1.8% difference).

</details>


### [221] [When Should an AI Scientist Stop? Verifiable Experiment Steering and Refusal for Autonomous Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07576)
*Neel Tushar Shah,Manglam Kartik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARTOGRAPH是一个用于AI科学家的验证层，通过未解析子空间实验引导（select）、显式模糊闭合（resolve）和基于残差的库不充分性检测（refuse）三组件，在局部线性-高斯桥接下实现实验设计与验证。在多个测试床中表现出色，并能识别和撤销错误的库外机制识别，同时在A-Lab材料系统审计中成功识别所有后续被标记为不确定的阳性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统缺乏对实验设计决策的严格验证机制，尤其在面对未解析子空间、模糊闭合以及库外机制时容易出现误判，需要一种统一的验证层来增强AI驱动的科学发现的可靠性。

Method: 提出CARTOGRAPH验证层，包含三个核心组件：(1) select - 基于未解析子空间的实验引导；(2) resolve - 显式模糊闭合；(3) refuse - 基于残差的库不充分性检测。在局部线性-高斯桥接假设下，将未解析投影定义为各向同性的未解析Fisher信息迹，CARTOGRAPH-A则为精确的未解析A-最优规则，闭式EIG和Box-Hill作为局部比较器而非全局等价物出现。

Result: 在五个测试床中：(1) CARTOGRAPH-A在d=8的复制结构化级联中以129胜/0平/15负击败原始投影（p<10^-21）；(2) 框架初步识别三个库外药代动力学机制，随后因残差暴露结构不适应而撤销识别，而一个受扰动的库内对照始终被识别；(3) 在低维药代动力学和过滤EPA设置中，理论预测的接近平局与观察一致；(4) 在A-Lab材料系统的40项阳性声明回顾审计中，拒绝守卫标记了所有4项后续被人工复核标记为不确定的声明，同时通过了32/36项已确认声明。

Conclusion: CARTOGRAPH为AI科学家提供了一个有效的验证层，能够增强实验设计的严谨性，识别并纠正错误的库外机制识别，并在实际自主材料发现系统中有效筛选可疑结果，展示了在AI驱动科学发现中集成严格验证机制的价值。

Abstract: We present CARTOGRAPH, a verification layer for AI scientists that couples unresolved-subspace experiment steering (select), explicit ambiguity closure (resolve), and residual-based library inadequacy detection (refuse). Under a local linear-Gaussian bridge, raw unresolved projection is the isotropic unresolved Fisher-information trace, while CARTOGRAPH-A is the exact unresolved A-optimal rule; closed-form EIG and Box-Hill arise as local comparators rather than global equivalents. Across five testbeds, CARTOGRAPH-A beats raw projection 129W/0T/15L at d = 8 (p < 10^-21) in a replicated structured cascade. More distinctively, the framework tentatively identifies three out-of-library pharmacokinetic mechanisms and then revokes those identifications as residuals expose structural misfit, while one perturbed in-library control stays identified throughout. In low-dimensional pharmacokinetic and filtered EPA settings, near-ties against disagreement are predicted by theory and observed. Finally, in a retrospective audit of 40 positive claims from the published A-Lab autonomous materials system, the refuse guard flags all 4 claims later marked inconclusive under manual reanalysis while passing 32/36 confirmed claims. Code is available at https://github.com/ai4science-boed/cartograph.git

</details>


### [222] [MST-Direct at Scale: Multivariate and Conditional Geostatistical Simulation via Sinkhorn Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07578)
*Tcharlies Bachmann Schmitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了MST-Direct方法，通过稀疏Sinkhorn匹配器、多变量扩展和硬数据条件化，实现了从双变量无条件小网格到多变量条件大网格的地统计模拟，在保持联合分布精确的前提下大幅提升可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决原始MST-Direct方法的三个主要限制：无法扩展到数千节点以上的大规模网格、仅支持双变量场景、无法处理条件模拟中的硬数据约束。

Method: (i) 提出稀疏候选限制Sinkhorn匹配器，内存复杂度为O(nC)；(ii) 将目标值元组匹配到独立的FFT-MA高斯骨干上，以再现指定变差函数；(iii) 通过克里金法对骨干进行条件化，固定观测数据元组的位置，同时保持置换传输计划以精确保留多元联合分布。

Result: 在六变量异方差强非线性参考分布下，无条件(200x200)和条件(100x100, 200个硬数据样本)实验中，MST-Direct实现了零直方图误差、精确拟合硬数据、准确再现空间相关结构，优于PPMT的近似结果。

Conclusion: MST-Direct成功扩展至大规模多变量条件模拟场景，为多元地统计模拟提供了一种可扩展且精确的最优传输方法。

Abstract: This paper extends MST-Direct, a Matching-via-Sinkhorn-Transport approach for multivariate geostatistical simulation, from the original bivariate, unconditional, small-grid formulation to multivariate, conditional, and large-grid settings. We address the three main limitations identified in the original work: (i) scalability beyond a few thousand nodes through a sparse, candidate-restricted Sinkhorn matcher with O(nC) memory complexity; (ii) extension to multiple variables by matching target value tuples onto an independent FFT-MA Gaussian backbone that reproduces a prescribed variogram; and (iii) hard-data conditioning by fixing observed data tuples at their spatial locations while conditioning the backbone through kriging. Because the transport plan remains a permutation of the target tuples, the multivariate joint distribution is preserved exactly.
  The method is validated using the same six-variate, heteroscedastic, strongly nonlinear reference distribution employed in Direct Multivariate Simulation (DMS), under both unconditional (200x200) and conditional (100x100, 200 hard-data samples) scenarios, and is benchmarked against the Projection Pursuit Multivariate Transform (PPMT). Results show that MST-Direct reproduces the joint distribution with zero histogram error, exactly honours hard data, and accurately reproduces the prescribed spatial correlation structure, whereas PPMT remains an approximation.
  Index Terms-Optimal transport, Sinkhorn algorithm, geostatistical simulation, multivariate simulation.

</details>


### [223] [Training-Inference Kernel Contracts: Bounding Divergence in Post-Training and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07581)
*Bruce Changlong Xu,Lan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"核合约"(kernel contracts)框架，用于规范训练核与推理核在有限精度下产生分布差异时的可接受偏差范围，包含数值、统计、运行时和可观测性条款，并推导了从logit漂移到策略梯度偏差的误差链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代后训练管线中，同一策略在网络训练核与推理核上执行时，由于有限精度优化差异，会在权重相同的情况下产生不同分布，且这些差异集中在聚合基准测试未充分覆盖的切片上，现有方法缺乏系统化的差异管控机制。

Method: 提出合约框架C=(N,S,R,O,Pi)，包含数值(N)、统计(S)、运行时(R)和可观测性(O)条款，以及违规升级策略(Pi)。推导从logit漂移到总变差距离、再到有界奖励漂移的误差链，并在RL后训练中通过per-token重要性比率漂移在支撑和范数假设下给出策略梯度偏差的理论界。还描述了四阶段晋升管线、在线路由循环和YAML DSL。

Result: 本文是框架与词汇论文，未报告生产规模的经验验证结果，但提供了完整的理论误差传递链和实现规范。

Conclusion: 核合约为训练-推理分布差异提供了形式化的规范框架，为后续的实证研究和工程落地奠定了理论基础和标准化接口。

Abstract: A modern post-training pipeline often writes one symbol for its policy, pi_theta, while evaluating it through two different programs: a training kernel optimized for autograd and an inference kernel optimized for low-precision, fused, dynamically batched serving. In finite precision, these kernels can induce different distributions at identical weights, with the gap concentrated on slices that aggregate benchmarks under-represent. This paper proposes kernel contracts: a contract-first framework for specifying acceptable divergence between K_train and K_inf. A contract C = (N, S, R, O, Pi) combines numerical, statistical, runtime, and observability clauses with an escalation policy from violations to routing actions. We derive a chain of bounds from logit drift to total-variation distance to bounded reward drift, and specialize it to RL post-training, where per-token importance-ratio drift yields a bound on policy-gradient bias under explicit support and norm assumptions. We also describe a four-stage promotion pipeline, online routing loop, and minimal YAML DSL for contract artifacts. This is a framework and vocabulary paper; we do not report production-scale empirical validation.

</details>


### [224] [Customer Churn Prediction on Structured Data Using FT-Transformer and Stacking Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07582)
